

relation Type_alpha: typeIR'''', typeIR'''''

. Case analysis on typeIR''''
 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type baseTypeIR
  ... Let `baseTypeIR` be `typeIR''''` as baseTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type baseTypeIR
  ... Let `baseTypeIR'` be `typeIR'''''` as baseTypeIR
  ... Assert that (baseTypeIR = baseTypeIR')
  ... Group basetype: `baseTypeIR` as typeIR, `baseTypeIR` as typeIR

   .... The relation holds

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type nameTypeIR
  ... Let `(tid tid)` be `typeIR''''` as nameTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type nameTypeIR
  ... Let `(tid tid')` be `typeIR'''''` as nameTypeIR
  ... Assert that (tid = tid')
  ... Group nametype: `(tid tid)` as typeIR, `(tid tid)` as typeIR

   .... The relation holds

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type specializedTypeIR
  ... Let `(polyTypeDefIR_a < typeIR_a* >)` be `typeIR''''` as specializedTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type specializedTypeIR
  ... Let `(polyTypeDefIR_b < typeIR_b* >)` be `typeIR'''''` as specializedTypeIR
  ... Group spectype: `(polyTypeDefIR_a < typeIR_a* >)` as typeIR, `(polyTypeDefIR_b < typeIR_b* >)` as typeIR

   .... Let `typeIR_a_spec` be `$specialize_typeDef(`polyTypeDefIR_a` as typeDefIR, typeIR_a*)`
   .... Let `typeIR_b_spec` be `$specialize_typeDef(`polyTypeDefIR_b` as typeDefIR, typeIR_b*)`
   .... If [`typeIR_a_spec` and `typeIR_b_spec` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_nominal_typeIR(typeIR_a_spec)` /\ `$is_nominal_typeIR(typeIR_b_spec)`
    ..... If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), for each `typeIR_a` in `typeIR_a*` and `typeIR_b` in `typeIR_b*`, then
     ...... The relation holds
    ..... Assert that ~`$is_nominal_typeIR(typeIR_a_spec)` /\ ~`$is_nominal_typeIR(typeIR_b_spec)`
    ..... The relation holds
. Group typedeftype: typeIR, typeIR'

 .. Assert that `typeIR''''` has type typedefTypeIR
 .. Let `(typedef _tid typeIR_a)` be `typeIR''''` as typedefTypeIR
 .. If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR'''''` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
  ... The relation holds
 .. Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type typedefTypeIR
 .. Let `(typedef _tid typeIR_b)` be `typeIR'''''` as typedefTypeIR
 .. If [`typeIR''''` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
  ... The relation holds
. Case analysis on typeIR''''
 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type newTypeIR
  ... Let `(type tid typeIR_a)` be `typeIR''''` as newTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type newTypeIR
  ... Let `(type tid' typeIR_b)` be `typeIR'''''` as newTypeIR
  ... Assert that (tid = tid')
  ... Group newtype: `(type tid typeIR_a)` as typeIR, `(type tid typeIR_b)` as typeIR

   .... If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
    ..... The relation holds

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type listTypeIR
  ... Let `(list< typeIR_a >)` be `typeIR''''` as listTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type listTypeIR
  ... Let `(list< typeIR_b >)` be `typeIR'''''` as listTypeIR
  ... Group listtype: `(list< typeIR_a >)` as typeIR, `(list< typeIR_b >)` as typeIR

   .... If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type tupleTypeIR

  ... Let `(tuple< typeIR_b* >)` be `typeIR'''''` as tupleTypeIR
  ... Group tupletype: `(tuple< typeIR_a* >)` as typeIR, `(tuple< typeIR_b* >)` as typeIR

   .... If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), for each `typeIR_a` in `typeIR_a*` and `typeIR_b` in `typeIR_b*`, then
    ..... The relation holds

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type headerStackTypeIR
  ... Let `(typeIR_a [ n_s ])` be `typeIR''''` as headerStackTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type headerStackTypeIR
  ... Let `(typeIR_b [ n_s' ])` be `typeIR'''''` as headerStackTypeIR
  ... Assert that (n_s = n_s')
  ... Group stacktype: `(typeIR_a [ n_s ])` as typeIR, `(typeIR_b [ n_s ])` as typeIR

   .... If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type structTypeIR

  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type headerUnionTypeIR

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type enumTypeIR
  ... Let `enumTypeIR` be `typeIR''''` as enumTypeIR
  ... Case analysis on enumTypeIR
   .... Case `enumTypeIR` matches pattern `ENUM%{%}`
    ..... Let `(enum tid { id_f* })` be enumTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type enumTypeIR
    ..... Let `enumTypeIR'` be `typeIR'''''` as enumTypeIR
    ..... Assert that enumTypeIR' matches pattern `ENUM%{%}`
    ..... Let `(enum tid' { id_f'* })` be enumTypeIR'
    ..... Assert that (id_f = id_f'), for each `id_f` in `id_f*` and `id_f'` in `id_f'*`
    ..... Assert that (tid = tid')
    ..... Group enumtype: `(enum tid { id_f* })` as typeIR, `(enum tid { id_f* })` as typeIR

     ...... The relation holds

   .... Case `enumTypeIR` matches pattern `ENUM%#%{%}`
    ..... Let `(enum tid # typeIR_a { (id_f = value_f ;)* })` be enumTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type enumTypeIR
    ..... Let `enumTypeIR'` be `typeIR'''''` as enumTypeIR
    ..... Assert that enumTypeIR' matches pattern `ENUM%#%{%}`
    ..... Let `(enum tid' # typeIR_b { (id_f' = value_f' ;)* })` be enumTypeIR'
    ..... Assert that (id_f = id_f'), for each `id_f` in `id_f*` and `id_f'` in `id_f'*`
    ..... Assert that (tid = tid')
    ..... Assert that (value_f = value_f'), for each `value_f` in `value_f*` and `value_f'` in `value_f'*`
    ..... Group serenumtype: `(enum tid # typeIR_a { (id_f = value_f ;)* })` as typeIR, `(enum tid # typeIR_b { (id_f = value_f ;)* })` as typeIR

     ...... If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
      ....... The relation holds

 .. Case `typeIR''''` has type externObjectTypeIR
  ... Let `(extern tid ({ (rid_a : routineTypeDefIR_a)* }))` be `typeIR''''` as externObjectTypeIR
  ... Assert that `typeIR'''''` has type externObjectTypeIR
  ... Let `(extern tid' ({ (rid_b : routineTypeDefIR_b)* }))` be `typeIR'''''` as externObjectTypeIR
  ... Assert that (tid = tid')
  ... Group externtype: `(extern tid ({ (rid_a : routineTypeDefIR_a)* }))` as typeIR, `(extern tid ({ (rid_b : routineTypeDefIR_b)* }))` as typeIR

   .... Assert that `$eq_set<rid>(({ rid_a* }), ({ rid_b* }))`
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR?*` be the list of `routineTypeDefIR?`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `routineTypeDefIR?` be `$find_map<rid, routineTypeDefIR>(({ (rid_a : routineTypeDefIR_a)* }), rid_a)`
   .... for each `rid_a` in `rid_a*`
   .... Assert that routineTypeDefIR? matches pattern (_), for each `routineTypeDefIR?` in `routineTypeDefIR?*`
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR_a'*` be the list of `routineTypeDefIR_a'`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `?(routineTypeDefIR_a')` be routineTypeDefIR?
   .... for each `routineTypeDefIR?` in `routineTypeDefIR?*`
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR'?*` be the list of `routineTypeDefIR'?`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `routineTypeDefIR'?` be `$find_map<rid, routineTypeDefIR>(({ (rid_b : routineTypeDefIR_b)* }), rid_a)`
   .... for each `rid_a` in `rid_a*`
   .... Assert that routineTypeDefIR'? matches pattern (_), for each `routineTypeDefIR'?` in `routineTypeDefIR'?*`
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR_b'*` be the list of `routineTypeDefIR_b'`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `?(routineTypeDefIR_b')` be routineTypeDefIR'?
   .... for each `routineTypeDefIR'?` in `routineTypeDefIR'?*`
   .... If [`routineTypeDefIR_a'` and `routineTypeDefIR_b'` are the same type](RoutineTypeDef_alpha), for each `routineTypeDefIR_a'` in `routineTypeDefIR_a'*` and `routineTypeDefIR_b'` in `routineTypeDefIR_b'*`, then
    ..... The relation holds

relation RoutineType_alpha: routineTypeIR, routineTypeIR'

. Case analysis on routineTypeIR
 .. Case `routineTypeIR` has type functionTypeIR
  ... Let `functionTypeIR` be `routineTypeIR` as functionTypeIR
  ... Case analysis on functionTypeIR
   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `BUILTIN_FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(builtin_function( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `routineTypeIR'` has type functionTypeIR
    ..... Let `functionTypeIR'` be `routineTypeIR'` as functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that functionTypeIR' matches pattern `BUILTIN_FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(builtin_function( parameterTypeIR_b* )-> typeIR_ret_b)` be functionTypeIR'
    ..... Group builtinfunction: `(builtin_function( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` as routineTypeIR, `(builtin_function( parameterTypeIR_b* )-> typeIR_ret_b)` as routineTypeIR

     ...... If [`parameterTypeIR_a` and `parameterTypeIR_b` are the same type](ParameterType_alpha), for each `parameterTypeIR_a` in `parameterTypeIR_a*` and `parameterTypeIR_b` in `parameterTypeIR_b*`, then
      ....... If [`typeIR_ret_a` and `typeIR_ret_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
       ........ The relation holds

   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(function( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `routineTypeIR'` has type functionTypeIR
    ..... Let `functionTypeIR'` be `routineTypeIR'` as functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that functionTypeIR' matches pattern `FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(function( parameterTypeIR_b* )-> typeIR_ret_b)` be functionTypeIR'
    ..... Group function: `(function( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` as routineTypeIR, `(function( parameterTypeIR_b* )-> typeIR_ret_b)` as routineTypeIR

     ...... If [`parameterTypeIR_a` and `parameterTypeIR_b` are the same type](ParameterType_alpha), for each `parameterTypeIR_a` in `parameterTypeIR_a*` and `parameterTypeIR_b` in `parameterTypeIR_b*`, then
      ....... If [`typeIR_ret_a` and `typeIR_ret_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
       ........ The relation holds

   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `ACTION(%)`
    ..... Let `(action( parameterTypeIR_a* ))` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `routineTypeIR'` has type functionTypeIR
    ..... Let `functionTypeIR'` be `routineTypeIR'` as functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that functionTypeIR' matches pattern `ACTION(%)`
    ..... Let `(action( parameterTypeIR_b* ))` be functionTypeIR'
    ..... Group action: `(action( parameterTypeIR_a* ))` as routineTypeIR, `(action( parameterTypeIR_b* ))` as routineTypeIR

     ...... If [`parameterTypeIR_a` and `parameterTypeIR_b` are the same type](ParameterType_alpha), for each `parameterTypeIR_a` in `parameterTypeIR_a*` and `parameterTypeIR_b` in `parameterTypeIR_b*`, then
      ....... The relation holds

   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `EXTERN_FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(extern_function( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `routineTypeIR'` has type functionTypeIR
    ..... Let `functionTypeIR'` be `routineTypeIR'` as functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that functionTypeIR' matches pattern `EXTERN_FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(extern_function( parameterTypeIR_b* )-> typeIR_ret_b)` be functionTypeIR'
    ..... Group externfunction: `(extern_function( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` as routineTypeIR, `(extern_function( parameterTypeIR_b* )-> typeIR_ret_b)` as routineTypeIR

     ...... If [`parameterTypeIR_a` and `parameterTypeIR_b` are the same type](ParameterType_alpha), for each `parameterTypeIR_a` in `parameterTypeIR_a*` and `parameterTypeIR_b` in `parameterTypeIR_b*`, then
      ....... If [`typeIR_ret_a` and `typeIR_ret_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
       ........ The relation holds

 .. Case `routineTypeIR` has type methodTypeIR
  ... Let `methodTypeIR` be `routineTypeIR` as methodTypeIR
  ... Case analysis on methodTypeIR
   .... Case `methodTypeIR` matches pattern `BUILTIN_METHOD(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(builtin_method( parameterTypeIR_a* )-> typeIR_ret_a)` be methodTypeIR
    ..... Assert that `routineTypeIR'` has type methodTypeIR
    ..... Let `methodTypeIR'` be `routineTypeIR'` as methodTypeIR
    ..... Assert that methodTypeIR' matches pattern `BUILTIN_METHOD(%)->%`

relation RoutineTypeDef_alpha: routineTypeDefIR, routineTypeDefIR'

. Case analysis on routineTypeDefIR
 .. Case `routineTypeDefIR` has type routineTypeIR
  ... Let `routineTypeIR_a` be `routineTypeDefIR` as routineTypeIR
  ... Assert that `routineTypeDefIR'` has type routineTypeIR
  ... Let `routineTypeIR_b` be `routineTypeDefIR'` as routineTypeIR
  ... Group mono: `routineTypeIR_a` as routineTypeDefIR, `routineTypeIR_b` as routineTypeDefIR

   .... If [`routineTypeIR_a` and `routineTypeIR_b` are the same type](RoutineType_alpha), then
    ..... The relation holds

 .. Case `routineTypeDefIR` has type polyRoutineTypeDefIR
  ... Let `(routineTypeIR_a < tid_a* , tid_hidden_a* >)` be `routineTypeDefIR` as polyRoutineTypeDefIR
  ... Assert that `routineTypeDefIR'` has type polyRoutineTypeDefIR
  ... Let `(routineTypeIR_b < tid_b* , tid_hidden_b* >)` be `routineTypeDefIR'` as polyRoutineTypeDefIR
  ... Group poly: `(routineTypeIR_a < tid_a* , tid_hidden_a* >)` as routineTypeDefIR, `(routineTypeIR_b < tid_b* , tid_hidden_b* >)` as routineTypeDefIR

   .... Assert that (|tid_a*| = |tid_b*|)
   .... Assert that (|tid_hidden_a*| = |tid_hidden_b*|)
   .... Let `tid_fresh*` be `$fresh_tids(|tid_a*| + |tid_hidden_a*|)`
   .... Let `tid_a'*` be tid_a* ++ tid_hidden_a*
   .... Let `theta_a` be ({ (tid_a' : `(tid tid_fresh)` as typeIR)* })
   .... Let `routineTypeIR_a_subst` be `$subst_routineType(theta_a, routineTypeIR_a)`
   .... Let `tid_b'*` be tid_b* ++ tid_hidden_b*
   .... Let `theta_b` be ({ (tid_b' : `(tid tid_fresh)` as typeIR)* })
   .... Let `routineTypeIR_b_subst` be `$subst_routineType(theta_b, routineTypeIR_b)`
   .... If [`routineTypeIR_a_subst` and `routineTypeIR_b_subst` are the same type](RoutineType_alpha), then
    ..... The relation holds

relation ParameterType_wf: bound, (direction typeIR _id value''?)

. Assert that direction matches pattern ``EMPTY`
. Case analysis on value''?
 .. Case `value''?` matches pattern ()
  ... Group default-empty-extern: bound, (() typeIR _id ?())

   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_extern_object_typeIR(`$canon(typeIR)`)`
    ..... The relation holds

 .. Case `value''?` matches pattern (_)
  ... Let `?(value)` be value''?
  ... Group default-some-extern: bound, (() typeIR _id ?(value))

   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_extern_object_typeIR(`$canon(typeIR)`)`
    ..... The relation holds
. Case analysis on value''?
 .. Case `value''?` matches pattern ()
  ... Group default-none-not-extern: bound, (direction typeIR _id ?())

   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that ~`$is_extern_object_typeIR(`$canon(typeIR)`)`
    ..... The relation holds

 .. Case `value''?` matches pattern (_)
  ... Let `?(value)` be value''?
  ... Group default-some-not-extern: bound, (direction typeIR _id ?(value))

   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that ~`$is_extern_object_typeIR(`$canon(typeIR)`)`
    ..... Case analysis on direction
     ...... Case `direction` matches pattern `IN`
      ....... The relation holds

     ...... Case `direction` matches pattern ``EMPTY`
      ....... The relation holds

 .. Let `_direction*` be the list of `_direction` and `_typeIR*` be the list of `_typeIR` and `_value?*` be the list of `_value?` and `id*` be the list of `id`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `(_direction _typeIR id _value?)` be parameterTypeIR
 .. for each `parameterTypeIR` in `parameterTypeIR*`

 .. If [`parameterTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](ParameterType_wf), for each `parameterTypeIR` in `parameterTypeIR*`, then
  ... The relation holds

 .. Case `routineTypeIR` has type functionTypeIR

       ........ Let `(direction typeIR _id _value?)` be parameterTypeIR

      ....... Assert that `$nestable_function(direction, typeIR)`, for each `direction` in `direction*` and `typeIR` in `typeIR*`

       ........ Let `(direction typeIR _id _value?)` be parameterTypeIR
      ....... for each `parameterTypeIR` in `parameterTypeIR*`
      ....... Assert that `$directionless_trailing(direction*)`
      ....... Assert that `$nestable_action(direction, typeIR)`, for each `direction` in `direction*` and `typeIR` in `typeIR*`

    ..... Let `(builtin_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be methodTypeIR
    ..... Group builtinmethod: bound, `(builtin_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR

   .... Case analysis on booleanLiteral
    ..... Case `booleanLiteral` matches pattern `TRUE`

    ..... Case `booleanLiteral` matches pattern `FALSE`

    ..... Case `enumTypeIR` matches pattern `ENUM%{%}`
     ...... Let `(enum tid { id_member* })` be enumTypeIR
     ...... Assert that nameIR is in id_member*

    ..... Case `enumTypeIR` matches pattern `ENUM%#%{%}`
     ...... Let `(enum tid # typeIR { (id_member = value_member ;)* })` be enumTypeIR

  ... Assert that `memberAccessBaseIR` has type typedExpressionIR
  ... Let `typedExpressionIR_base` be `memberAccessBaseIR` as typedExpressionIR
  ... Assert that (nameIR = "size")
  ... Group accessExpressionIR-memberAccessExpressionIR-typedExpressionIR-stack-size: p, TC, (`(`typedExpressionIR_base` as memberAccessBaseIR . "size")` as expressionIR # (( _typeIR _ctk )))

    ..... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern []

     ...... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk' )))` be typedExpressionIR_base

    ..... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern []

    ..... Let `(( routineTargetIR ))` be routineTargetIR
    ..... Group callExpressionIR-parenthesized: p, TC, (`((( routineTargetIR )) < typeArgumentIR* >( argumentIR* ))` as expressionIR # (( typeIR ctk )))

     ...... Result in value

relation Type_ok: p, TC, typeOrVoid

. Case analysis on typeOrVoid
 .. Case `typeOrVoid` has type baseType

  ... Case analysis on baseType

(TypeArgument_ok: p TC |- typeArgument : typeIR_arg # tid_fresh*)

relation Sub_expl: typeIR_a, typeIR_b

. Group : typeIR_a, typeIR_b

 .. Let `typeIR_a_canon` be `$canon(typeIR_a)`
 .. Let `typeIR_b_canon` be `$canon(typeIR_b)`
 .. If [`typeIR_a_canon` can be explicitly cast to `typeIR_b_canon` after canonicalization](Sub_expl_canon), then
  ... The relation holds

relation Sub_expl_canon: typeIR_a, typeIR_b

. Group equals: typeIR_a, typeIR_b

 .. If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
  ... The relation holds
. Group not-equals: typeIR_a, typeIR_b

 .. If [`typeIR_a` and `typeIR_b` are the same type](Type_alpha) does not hold, then
  ... If [`typeIR_a` can be explicitly cast to `typeIR_b` when they are not the same type after canonicalization](Sub_expl_canon_neq), then
   .... The relation holds

     ...... Assert that `typeIR'''''''` has type numberTypeIR

     ...... Assert that (1 = nat)

     ...... The relation holds
     ...... Assert that (`(int)` as typeIR = typeIR''''''')
     ...... The relation holds
     ...... Assert that (`(int< nat >)` as typeIR = typeIR''''''')
     ...... The relation holds
     ...... Assert that `typeIR'''''''` has type numberTypeIR
     ...... Let `numberTypeIR` be `typeIR'''''''` as numberTypeIR

     ...... The relation holds

  ... Group returnStatement: (local), TC, f, `returnStatement` as statement

   .... Case analysis on returnStatement
    ..... Case `returnStatement` matches pattern `RETURN;`
     ...... Assert that (?(`(void)` as typeIR) = `$find_return_type(TC')`)
     ...... Result in TC', (ret), `(return;)` as statementIR

    ..... Case `returnStatement` matches pattern `RETURN%;`
     ...... Let `(return expression ;)` be returnStatement
     ...... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC'`](Expr_ok)
     ...... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR
     ...... Let `typeIR'?` be `$find_return_type(TC')`
     ...... Assert that typeIR'? matches pattern (_)
     ...... Let `?(typeIR_ret)` be typeIR'?
     ...... Let `typedExpressionIR'?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR, typeIR_ret)`
     ...... Assert that typedExpressionIR'? matches pattern (_)
     ...... Let `?(typedExpressionIR_cast)` be typedExpressionIR'?
     ...... Result in TC', (ret), `(return typedExpressionIR_cast ;)` as statementIR

 .. Case `statement` has type blockStatement
  ... Let `blockStatement` be `statement` as blockStatement
  ... Group blockStatement: (local), TC_0, f_0, `blockStatement` as statement

(Block_ok: TC' f' |- blockStatement : TC_1 f_1 blockStatementIR)
   .... Result in TC_1, f_1, `blockStatementIR` as statementIR

 .. Case `statement` has type switchStatement
  ... Let `(switch( expression_switch ){ switchCaseList })` be `statement` as switchStatement
  ... Group switch-table-apply: (local), TC, f, `(switch( expression_switch ){ switchCaseList })` as statement

   .... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_switch`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC'`](Expr_ok)
   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_switch _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_switch
   .... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_switch)`
   .... Assert that `typeIR` has type tableTypeIR
   .... Let `tableTypeIR` be `typeIR` as tableTypeIR
   .... Assert that tableTypeIR matches pattern `TABLE_ENUM%{%}`
   .... Let `(table_enum tid_table_enum { _id* })` be tableTypeIR
   .... Let `tid_table` be `$strip_prefix(`$strip_suffix(tid_table_enum, ")")`, "action_list(")`
   .... Let `switchCase*` be `$flatten_switchCaseList(switchCaseList)`
(SwitchCases_table_ok: TC' f' tid_table |- switchCase* : f_post switchCaseIR* # switchLabel*)
   .... Assert that [the elements of `switchLabel*` are distinct]($distinct_)
   .... Let `switchStatementIR` be (switch( typedExpressionIR_switch ){ switchCaseIR* })
   .... Result in TC', f_post, `switchStatementIR` as statementIR
  ... Group switch-general: (local), TC, f, `(switch( expression_switch ){ switchCaseList })` as statement

   .... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_switch`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC'`](Expr_ok)
   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_switch _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_switch
   .... Assert that `$compat_switch(typeIR_switch)`
   .... Let `switchCase*` be `$flatten_switchCaseList(switchCaseList)`
(SwitchCases_general_ok: TC' f' typeIR_switch |- switchCase* : f_post switchCaseIR* # switchLabel*)
   .... Assert that [the elements of `switchLabel*` are distinct]($distinct_)
   .... Let `switchStatementIR` be (switch( typedExpressionIR_switch ){ switchCaseIR* })
   .... Result in TC', f_post, `switchStatementIR` as statementIR

relation BlockElementStmt_ok: TC_0, f, blockElementStatement

. Case analysis on blockElementStatement
 .. Case `blockElementStatement` has type constantDeclaration
  ... Let `constantDeclaration` be `blockElementStatement` as constantDeclaration
  ... Group constantDeclaration: TC_0, f, `constantDeclaration` as blockElementStatement

   .... Let `Decl_ok` be [the result of typing ``constantDeclaration` as declaration`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0`,
and the modified typing context, respectively](Decl_ok)
   .... Assert that `declarationIR` has type constantDeclarationIR
   .... Let `constantDeclarationIR` be `declarationIR` as constantDeclarationIR
   .... Result in TC_1, f, `constantDeclarationIR` as blockElementStatementIR

 .. Case `blockElementStatement` has type variableDeclaration
  ... Let `(annotationList type name initializerOpt ;)` be `blockElementStatement` as variableDeclaration
  ... Group variableDeclaration: TC_0, f, `(annotationList type name initializerOpt ;)` as blockElementStatement

   .... Assert that initializerOpt matches pattern ``EMPTY`
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing ``type` as typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((local), TC_0)``](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_assignable_typeIR(typeIR)`
    ..... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
    ..... Let `TC_1` be `$add_var((local), TC_0, nameIR, ((inout) typeIR (dyn) ?()))`
    ..... Let `variableDeclarationIR` be (annotationList typeIR nameIR ?() ;)
    ..... Result in TC_1, f, `variableDeclarationIR` as blockElementStatementIR
   .... Assert that `initializerOpt` has type initializer
   .... Let `(= expression_init)` be `initializerOpt` as initializer
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing ``type` as typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((local), TC_0)``](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_assignable_typeIR(typeIR)`
    ..... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_init`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0`](Expr_ok)
    ..... Let `typedExpressionIR?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR_init, typeIR)`
    ..... Assert that typedExpressionIR? matches pattern (_)
    ..... Let `?(typedExpressionIR_init_cast)` be typedExpressionIR?
    ..... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
    ..... Let `TC_1` be `$add_var((local), TC_0, nameIR, ((inout) typeIR (dyn) ?()))`
    ..... Let `variableDeclarationIR` be (annotationList typeIR nameIR ?((= typedExpressionIR_init_cast)) ;)
    ..... Result in TC_1, f, `variableDeclarationIR` as blockElementStatementIR

 .. Case `blockElementStatement` has type statement
  ... Let `statement` be `blockElementStatement` as statement
  ... Group statement: TC_0, f, `statement` as blockElementStatement

(Stmt_ok: (local) TC_0 f |- statement : TC_1 f_post statementIR)
   .... Result in TC_1, f_post, `statementIR` as blockElementStatementIR

relation BlockElementStmts_ok: TC', f', blockElementStatement*

. Case analysis on blockElementStatement*
 .. Case `blockElementStatement*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: TC, f, []

   .... Result in TC', f', []

 .. Case `blockElementStatement*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `blockElementStatement_h :: blockElementStatement_t*` be blockElementStatement*
  ... Group cons: TC_0, f_0, blockElementStatement_h :: blockElementStatement_t*

(BlockElementStmt_ok: TC' f' |- blockElementStatement_h : TC_1 f_1 blockElementStatementIR_h)
(BlockElementStmts_ok: TC_1 f_1 |- blockElementStatement_t* : TC_2 f_2 blockElementStatementIR_t*)
   .... Result in TC_2, f_2, blockElementStatementIR_h :: blockElementStatementIR_t*

relation Block_ok: TC_0, f, (annotationList { blockElementStatementList })

. Group : TC_0, f, (annotationList { blockElementStatementList })

 .. Let `blockElementStatement*` be `$flatten_blockElementStatementList(blockElementStatementList)`
 .. Let `TC_1` be `$enter(TC_0)`
(BlockElementStmts_ok: TC_1 f |- blockElementStatement* : TC_2 f_post blockElementStatementIR*)
 .. Let `TC_3` be `$exit(TC_2)`
 .. Let `blockStatementIR` be (annotationList { blockElementStatementIR* })
 .. Result in TC_3, f_post, blockStatementIR

relation Parameter_ok: p, TC, (annotationList direction type name initializerOpt)

. Assert that initializerOpt matches pattern ``EMPTY`
. Group empty: p, TC, (annotationList direction type name ())

 .. Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing ``type` as typeOrVoid`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
 .. Let `bound` be `$union_set<tid>(`$bound(p, TC)`, ({ tid_fresh* }))`
 .. If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](Type_wf), then
  ... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
  ... Result in (annotationList direction typeIR nameIR ?()), tid_fresh*
. Assert that `initializerOpt` has type initializer
. Let `(= expression_init)` be `initializerOpt` as initializer
. Group initializer: p, TC, (annotationList direction type name `(= expression_init)` as initializerOpt)

 .. Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing ``type` as typeOrVoid`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
 .. Let `bound` be `$union_set<tid>(`$bound(p, TC)`, ({ tid_fresh* }))`
 .. If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](Type_wf), then
  ... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_init`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Expr_ok)
  ... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_init ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_init
  ... Assert that ctk matches pattern `LCTK`
  ... Let `typedExpressionIR?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR_init, typeIR)`
  ... Assert that typedExpressionIR? matches pattern (_)
  ... Let `?(typedExpressionIR_init_cast)` be typedExpressionIR?
  ... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
  ... Let `Eval_static` be [the static evaluation of `typedExpressionIR_init_cast`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Eval_static)
  ... Result in (annotationList direction typeIR nameIR ?((= value_init))), tid_fresh*

relation Parameters_ok: p, TC, parameter*

. Case analysis on parameter*
 .. Case `parameter*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p, TC, []

   .... Result in [], []

 .. Case `parameter*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `parameter_h :: parameter_t*` be parameter*
  ... Group cons: p, TC, parameter_h :: parameter_t*

   .... Let `Parameter_ok` be [`parameterIR_h` be the result of typing `parameter_h`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Parameter_ok)
   .... Let `Parameters_ok` be [`parameterIR_t*` be the result of typing `parameter_t*`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Parameters_ok)
   .... Result in parameterIR_h :: parameterIR_t*, tid_fresh_h* ++ tid_fresh_t*

relation ConstructorParameter_ok: p, TC, constructorParameter

. Group : p, TC, constructorParameter

 .. Let `Parameter_ok` be [`constructorParameterIR` be the result of typing `constructorParameter`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Parameter_ok)
 .. Result in constructorParameterIR, tid_fresh*

relation ConstructorParameters_ok: p, TC, constructorParameter*

. Group : p, TC, constructorParameter*

 .. Let `Parameters_ok` be [`constructorParameterIR*` be the result of typing `constructorParameter*`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Parameters_ok)
 .. Result in constructorParameterIR*, tid_fresh*

relation ExternMethod_ok: TC_0, tid_extern, methodPrototype

. Case analysis on methodPrototype
 .. Case `methodPrototype` matches pattern `%%;`
  ... Let `(annotationList (typeOrVoid name typeParameterListOpt ( parameterList )) ;)` be methodPrototype
  ... Group non-abstract: TC_0, tid_extern, (annotationList (typeOrVoid name typeParameterListOpt ( parameterList )) ;)

   .... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
   .... Assert that (nameIR =/= tid_extern)
   .... Let `typeParameter*` be `$flatten_typeParameterListOpt(typeParameterListOpt)`
   .... Let `tid_expl*` be the list of `tid_expl`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `tid_expl` be `$name(typeParameter)`
   .... for each `typeParameter` in `typeParameter*`
   .... Let `TC_1` be `$add_types((local), TC_0, tid_expl*, `(tid tid_expl)` as typeDefIR*)`
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing `typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_1`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... Let `TC_2` be TC_1[local.kind = (extern_method-> typeIR_ret)]
   .... Let `parameter*` be `$flatten_parameterList(parameterList)`
   .... Let `Parameters_ok` be [`parameterIR*` be the result of typing `parameter*`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_2`](Parameters_ok)
   .... Let `parameterTypeIR*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `parameterTypeIR` be `$parameterIR(parameterIR)`
   .... for each `parameterIR` in `parameterIR*`
   .... Let `TC_3` be `$add_parameters((local), TC_2, parameterTypeIR*)`
   .... Let `rid` be `$rid(name, parameterList)`
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (extern_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR` be `(`methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >)` as routineTypeDefIR
   .... If [`routineTypeDefIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((block), TC_0)``](RoutineTypeDef_wf), then
    ..... Let `TC_4` be `$add_routine_overload((block), TC_0, rid, routineTypeDefIR)`
    ..... Let `methodPrototypeIR` be (annotationList (typeIR_ret nameIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >( parameterIR* )) ;)
    ..... Result in TC_4, methodPrototypeIR

 .. Case `methodPrototype` matches pattern `%ABSTRACT%;`
  ... Let `(annotationList abstract (typeOrVoid name typeParameterListOpt ( parameterList )) ;)` be methodPrototype
  ... Group abstract: TC_0, tid_extern, (annotationList abstract (typeOrVoid name typeParameterListOpt ( parameterList )) ;)

   .... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
   .... Assert that (nameIR =/= tid_extern)
   .... Let `typeParameter*` be `$flatten_typeParameterListOpt(typeParameterListOpt)`
   .... Let `tid_expl*` be the list of `tid_expl`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `tid_expl` be `$name(typeParameter)`
   .... for each `typeParameter` in `typeParameter*`
   .... Let `TC_1` be `$add_types((local), TC_0, tid_expl*, `(tid tid_expl)` as typeDefIR*)`
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing `typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_1`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... Let `TC_2` be TC_1[local.kind = (extern_methodabstract-> typeIR_ret)]
   .... Let `parameter*` be `$flatten_parameterList(parameterList)`
   .... Let `Parameters_ok` be [`parameterIR*` be the result of typing `parameter*`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_2`](Parameters_ok)
   .... Let `parameterTypeIR*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `parameterTypeIR` be `$parameterIR(parameterIR)`
   .... for each `parameterIR` in `parameterIR*`
   .... Let `TC_3` be `$add_parameters((local), TC_2, parameterTypeIR*)`
   .... Let `rid` be `$rid(name, parameterList)`
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (extern_methodabstract( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR` be `(`methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >)` as routineTypeDefIR
   .... If [`routineTypeDefIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((block), TC_0)``](RoutineTypeDef_wf), then
    ..... Let `TC_4` be `$add_routine_overload((block), TC_0, rid, routineTypeDefIR)`
    ..... Let `methodPrototypeIR` be (annotationList abstract (typeIR_ret nameIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >( parameterIR* )) ;)
    ..... Result in TC_4, methodPrototypeIR

relation ExternMethods_ok: TC', tid_extern, methodPrototype*

. Case analysis on methodPrototype*
 .. Case `methodPrototype*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: TC, tid_extern, []

   .... Result in TC', []

 .. Case `methodPrototype*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `methodPrototype_h :: methodPrototype_t*` be methodPrototype*
  ... Group cons: TC_0, tid_extern, methodPrototype_h :: methodPrototype_t*

relation ExternConstructor_ok: TC_0, tid_extern, methodPrototype

. Assert that methodPrototype matches pattern `%%(%);`
. Let `(annotationList typeIdentifier ( parameterList );)` be methodPrototype
. Group : TC_0, tid_extern, (annotationList typeIdentifier ( parameterList );)

 .. Let `nameIR` be `$name(`typeIdentifier` as name)`
 .. Assert that (nameIR = tid_extern)
 .. Let `constructorParameter*` be `$flatten_parameterList(parameterList)`
(ConstructorParameters_ok: (block) TC_0 |- constructorParameter* : constructorParameterIR* # tid_impl*)
 .. Let `constructorParameterTypeIR*` be the list of `constructorParameterTypeIR`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `constructorParameterTypeIR` be `$constructorParameterIR(constructorParameterIR)`
 .. for each `constructorParameterIR` in `constructorParameterIR*`
 .. Let `typeDefIR'?` be `$find_type((global), TC_0, (` nameIR))`
 .. Assert that typeDefIR'? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?(typeDefIR)` be typeDefIR'?
 .. Assert that `typeDefIR` has type polyTypeDefIR
 .. Let `(typeIR_extern < tid_expl* , tid* >)` be `typeDefIR` as polyTypeDefIR
 .. Assert that tid* matches pattern []
 .. Let `cid` be `$cid(`typeIdentifier` as name, (( parameterList )))`
 .. Let `typeIR_extern_spec` be `((typeIR_extern < tid_expl* , [] >) < `(tid tid_expl)` as typeIR* >)` as typeIR
 .. Let `constructorTypeIR` be (constructor( constructorParameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_extern_spec)
 .. Let `constructorTypeDefIR` be (constructorTypeIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >)
 .. If [`constructorTypeDefIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((block), TC_0)``](ConstructorTypeDef_wf), then
  ... Let `TC_1` be `$add_constructor(TC_0, cid, constructorTypeDefIR)`
  ... Let `methodPrototypeIR` be (annotationList nameIR <, tid_impl* >( constructorParameterIR* );)
  ... Result in TC_1, methodPrototypeIR

relation ExternConstructors_ok: TC', tid_extern, methodPrototype*

. Case analysis on methodPrototype*
 .. Case `methodPrototype*` matches pattern []

relation ParserTransition_ok: TC_0, nameIR_state*, transitionStatement

. Case analysis on transitionStatement
 .. Case `transitionStatement` matches pattern ``EMPTY`
  ... Group empty: TC_0, nameIR_state*, ()

   .... Result in (transition ("reject" ;))

 .. Case `transitionStatement` matches pattern `TRANSITION%`
  ... Let `(transition stateExpression)` be transitionStatement
  ... Assert that stateExpression matches pattern `%;`
  ... Let `(name ;)` be stateExpression
  ... Group name: TC_0, nameIR_state*, (transition (name ;))

   .... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
   .... Assert that nameIR is in nameIR_state*
   .... Let `transitionStatementIR` be (transition (nameIR ;))
   .... Result in transitionStatementIR
  ... Assert that `stateExpression` has type selectExpression
  ... Let `(select( expressionList_key ){ selectCaseList })` be `stateExpression` as selectExpression
  ... Group switch: TC_0, nameIR_state*, (transition `(select( expressionList_key ){ selectCaseList })` as stateExpression)

   .... Let `expression_key*` be `$flatten_expressionList(expressionList_key)`
   .... Let `typedExpressionIR_key*` be the list of `typedExpressionIR_key`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_key`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0`](Expr_ok)
for each `expression_key` in `expression_key*`
   .... Let `_ctk*` be the list of `_ctk` and `_expressionIR*` be the list of `_expressionIR` and `typeIR_key*` be the list of `typeIR_key`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_key _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_key
   .... for each `typedExpressionIR_key` in `typedExpressionIR_key*`
   .... If [``(set< [typeIR_key] >)` as typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((local), TC_0)``](Type_wf), for each `typeIR_key` in `typeIR_key*`, then
    ..... Let `selectCase*` be `$flatten_selectCaseList(selectCaseList)`
(SelectCase_ok: TC_0 nameIR_state* typeIR_key* |- selectCase : selectCaseIR), for each `selectCase` in `selectCase*` and `selectCaseIR` in `selectCaseIR*`
    ..... Let `transitionStatementIR` be (transition `(select( typedExpressionIR_key* ){ selectCaseIR* })` as stateExpressionIR)
    ..... Result in transitionStatementIR

relation ParserStmt_ok: TC_0', parserStatement''

. Case analysis on parserStatement''
 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type constantDeclaration
  ... Let `constantDeclaration` be `parserStatement''` as constantDeclaration
  ... Group constantDeclaration: TC_0, `constantDeclaration` as parserStatement

   .... Let `Decl_ok` be [the result of typing ``constantDeclaration` as declaration`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0'`,
and the modified typing context, respectively](Decl_ok)
   .... Assert that `declarationIR` has type constantDeclarationIR
   .... Let `constantDeclarationIR` be `declarationIR` as constantDeclarationIR
   .... Result in TC_1, `constantDeclarationIR` as parserStatementIR

 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type variableDeclaration
  ... Let `(annotationList type name initializerOpt ;)` be `parserStatement''` as variableDeclaration
  ... Group variableDeclaration: TC_0, `(annotationList type name initializerOpt ;)` as parserStatement

   .... Assert that initializerOpt matches pattern ``EMPTY`
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing ``type` as typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0'`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((local), TC_0')``](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_assignable_typeIR(typeIR)`
    ..... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
    ..... Let `TC_1` be `$add_var((local), TC_0', nameIR, ((inout) typeIR (dyn) ?()))`
    ..... Let `variableDeclarationIR` be (annotationList typeIR nameIR ?() ;)
    ..... Result in TC_1, `variableDeclarationIR` as parserStatementIR
   .... Assert that `initializerOpt` has type initializer
   .... Let `(= expression_init)` be `initializerOpt` as initializer
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing ``type` as typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0'`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... If [`typeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((local), TC_0')``](Type_wf), then
    ..... Assert that `$is_assignable_typeIR(typeIR)`
    ..... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_init`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_0'`](Expr_ok)
    ..... Let `typedExpressionIR?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR_init, typeIR)`
    ..... Assert that typedExpressionIR? matches pattern (_)
    ..... Let `?(typedExpressionIR_init_cast)` be typedExpressionIR?
    ..... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
    ..... Let `TC_1` be `$add_var((local), TC_0', nameIR, ((inout) typeIR (dyn) ?()))`
    ..... Let `variableDeclarationIR` be (annotationList typeIR nameIR ?((= typedExpressionIR_init_cast)) ;)
    ..... Result in TC_1, `variableDeclarationIR` as parserStatementIR

 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type emptyStatement
  ... Let `emptyStatement` be `parserStatement''` as emptyStatement
  ... Group emptyStatement: TC, `emptyStatement` as parserStatement

(Stmt_ok: (local) TC_0' (cont) |- `emptyStatement` as statement : typingContext flow statementIR)
   .... Assert that (typingContext = TC_0')
   .... Assert that flow matches pattern `CONT`
   .... Assert that `statementIR` has type emptyStatementIR
   .... Let `emptyStatementIR` be `statementIR` as emptyStatementIR
   .... Result in TC_0', `emptyStatementIR` as parserStatementIR

 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type assignmentStatement
  ... Let `assignmentStatement` be `parserStatement''` as assignmentStatement
  ... Group assignmentStatement: TC_0, `assignmentStatement` as parserStatement

(Stmt_ok: (local) TC_0' (cont) |- `assignmentStatement` as statement : TC_1 flow statementIR)
   .... Assert that flow matches pattern `CONT`
   .... Assert that `statementIR` has type assignmentStatementIR
   .... Let `assignmentStatementIR` be `statementIR` as assignmentStatementIR
   .... Result in TC_1, `assignmentStatementIR` as parserStatementIR

 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type callStatement
  ... Let `callStatement` be `parserStatement''` as callStatement
  ... Group callStatement: TC_0, `callStatement` as parserStatement

(Stmt_ok: (local) TC_0' (cont) |- `callStatement` as statement : TC_1 flow statementIR)
   .... Assert that flow matches pattern `CONT`
   .... Assert that `statementIR` has type callStatementIR
   .... Let `callStatementIR` be `statementIR` as callStatementIR
   .... Result in TC_1, `callStatementIR` as parserStatementIR

 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type directApplicationStatement
  ... Let `directApplicationStatement` be `parserStatement''` as directApplicationStatement
  ... Group directApplicationStatement: TC_0, `directApplicationStatement` as parserStatement

(Stmt_ok: (local) TC_0' (cont) |- `directApplicationStatement` as statement : TC_1 flow statementIR)
   .... Assert that flow matches pattern `CONT`
   .... Assert that `statementIR` has type directApplicationStatementIR
   .... Let `directApplicationStatementIR` be `statementIR` as directApplicationStatementIR
   .... Result in TC_1, `directApplicationStatementIR` as parserStatementIR

 .. Case `parserStatement''` has type parserBlockStatement
  ... Let `(annotationList { parserStatementList })` be `parserStatement''` as parserBlockStatement

for each `argument` in `argument*`

relation TableEntry_priority_ok: TC, tableContext', TBLS, tableEntryPriority''?

. Case analysis on tableEntryPriority''?
 .. Case `tableEntryPriority''?` matches pattern ()
  ... Group non-specified: TC, tableContext, TBLS, ?()

   .... Assert that tableContext'.mode matches pattern `NOPRI`
   .... Result in tableContext', ?()
   .... Let `matchMode` be tableContext'.mode
   .... Assert that matchMode matches pattern `NOPRILPM%`
   .... Let `(noprilpm n)` be matchMode
   .... Assert that TBLS matches pattern `LPM%`
   .... Let `(lpm n_prefix)` be TBLS
   .... Result in tableContext', ?()
   .... Case analysis on tableContext'.mode
    ..... Case `tableContext'.mode` matches pattern `PRI`
     ...... Case analysis on tableContext'.priorities.values
      ....... Case tableContext'.priorities.values = []
       ........ Case analysis on tableContext'.priorities.largest_wins
        ......... Case true
         .......... Let `n_size` be tableContext'.entries.size
         .......... Let `n_delta` be tableContext'.priorities.delta
         .......... Let `int` be n_size - 1 * `n_delta` as int + `1` as int
         .......... Assert that `int` has type nat
         .......... Let `n` be `int` as nat
         .......... Let `TBLC_1` be `$add_table_priority(tableContext', n)`
         .......... Result in TBLC_1, ?((priority= (d `n` as int) :))

        ......... Case false
         .......... Let `TBLC_1` be `$add_table_priority(tableContext', 1)`
         .......... Result in TBLC_1, ?((priority= (d `1` as int) :))

      ....... Case tableContext'.priorities.values =/= []
       ........ Case analysis on tableContext'.priorities.largest_wins
        ......... Case true
         .......... Let `n_last` be `$find_table_priority_last(tableContext')`
         .......... Let `n_delta` be tableContext'.priorities.delta
         .......... Let `int` be n_last - n_delta
         .......... Assert that `int` has type nat
         .......... Let `n` be `int` as nat
         .......... Let `TBLC_1` be `$add_table_priority(tableContext', n)`
         .......... Result in TBLC_1, ?((priority= (d `n` as int) :))

        ......... Case false
         .......... Let `n_last` be `$find_table_priority_last(tableContext')`
         .......... Let `n_delta` be tableContext'.priorities.delta
         .......... Let `n` be n_last + n_delta
         .......... Let `TBLC_1` be `$add_table_priority(tableContext', n)`
         .......... Result in TBLC_1, ?((priority= (d `n` as int) :))

    ..... Case `tableContext'.mode` matches pattern `PRILPM`

        ......... Case false

relation Table_ok: TC, tableProperty*

. Group : TC, tableProperty*

 .. Assert that (`$count_table_keys(tableProperty*)` <= 1)
 .. Assert that (`$count_table_actions(tableProperty*)` = 1)
 .. Let `TBLC_0` be `$empty_tableContext`
(TableProperties_ok: TC TBLC_0 |- tableProperty* : TBLC_1 tablePropertyIR*)
 .. Result in TBLC_1, tablePropertyIR*

relation TableType_ok: TC_0, TBLC, name

. Group : TC_0, TBLC, name

 .. Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
 .. Let `tid_enum` be "action_list(" ++ nameIR ++ ")"
 .. Let `(prefixedNameIR_action, _parameterTypeIR*, _argumentListIR)*` be TBLC.actions
 .. Let `id_enum_field*` be the list of `id_enum_field`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `id_enum_field` be `$flatten_prefixedNameIR(prefixedNameIR_action)`
 .. for each `prefixedNameIR_action` in `prefixedNameIR_action*`
 .. Let `typeIR_table_enum` be `(table_enum tid_enum { id_enum_field* })` as typeIR
 .. Let `value_enum_field*` be the list of `value_enum_field`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `value_enum_field` be `(table_enum tid_enum . id_enum_field)` as value
 .. for each `id_enum_field` in `id_enum_field*`
 .. Let `varTypeIR_enum_field*` be the list of `varTypeIR_enum_field`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `varTypeIR_enum_field` be (() typeIR_table_enum (lctk) ?(value_enum_field))
 .. for each `value_enum_field` in `value_enum_field*`
 .. Let `TC_1` be `$add_vars((block), TC_0, tid_enum ++ "." ++ id_enum_field*, varTypeIR_enum_field*)`
 .. Let `tid_struct` be "apply_result(" ++ nameIR ++ ")"
 .. Let `typeIR_table_struct` be `(table_struct tid_struct { [(`(bool)` as typeIR "hit" ;), (`(bool)` as typeIR "miss" ;), (typeIR_table_enum "action_run" ;)] })` as typeIR
 .. Let `typeIR_table` be `(table nameIR # typeIR_table_struct)` as typeIR
 .. Result in TC_1, typeIR_table

and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)

    ..... Let `typeIR_package_spec` be `(polyTypeDefIR_package < `(tid tid_expl)` as typeIR* ++ `(tid tid_impl)` as typeIR* >)` as typeIR
    ..... Let `constructorTypeIR` be (constructor( constructorParameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_package_spec)
    ..... Let `constructorTypeDefIR` be (constructorTypeIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >)
    ..... If [`constructorTypeDefIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((global), TC_0)``](ConstructorTypeDef_wf), then
     ...... Let `TC_4` be `$add_constructor(TC_3, cid, constructorTypeDefIR)`
     ...... Let `packageTypeDeclarationIR` be (annotationList package nameIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >( constructorParameterIR* );)
     ...... Result in TC_4, `packageTypeDeclarationIR` as declarationIR

relation Decls_ok: p, TC', declaration*

. Case analysis on declaration*
 .. Case `declaration*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p, TC, []

   .... Result in TC', []

 .. Case `declaration*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `declaration_h :: declaration_t*` be declaration*
  ... Group cons: p, TC_0, declaration_h :: declaration_t*

   .... Let `Decl_ok` be [the result of typing `declaration_h`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC'`,
and the modified typing context, respectively](Decl_ok)
(Decls_ok: p TC_1 |- declaration_t* : TC_2 declarationIR_t*)
   .... Result in TC_2, declarationIR_h :: declarationIR_t*

relation Program_ok: p4program

. Group : p4program

 .. Let `declaration*` be `$flatten_p4program(p4program)`
 .. Let `TC_0` be `$empty_typingContext`
(Decls_ok: (global) TC_0 |- declaration* : TC_1 declarationIR*)
 .. Result in TC_1, (declarationIR* ;)

relation Call_convention_expr_ok: p, TC, actctxt, (direction typeIR_param _id _value?), typedExpressionIR_arg

. Assert that direction matches pattern `IN`
. Group in: p, TC, actctxt, ((in) typeIR_param _id _value?), typedExpressionIR_arg

 .. Let `typedExpressionIR?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR_arg, typeIR_param)`
 .. Assert that typedExpressionIR? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?(typedExpressionIR_arg_cast)` be typedExpressionIR?
 .. Result in typedExpressionIR_arg_cast
. Group out-inout: p, TC, actctxt, (direction typeIR_param _id _value?), typedExpressionIR_arg

 .. Case analysis on direction
  ... Case `direction` matches pattern `OUT`
   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_arg _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_arg
   .... If [`typeIR_param` and `typeIR_arg` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
    ..... If [`typedExpressionIR_arg` is a valid l-value, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Expr_lvalue_ok), then
     ...... Result in typedExpressionIR_arg

  ... Case `direction` matches pattern `INOUT`
   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_arg _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_arg
   .... If [`typeIR_param` and `typeIR_arg` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
    ..... If [`typedExpressionIR_arg` is a valid l-value, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Expr_lvalue_ok), then
     ...... Result in typedExpressionIR_arg
. Assert that direction matches pattern ``EMPTY`
. Group empty: p, TC, actctxt, (() typeIR_param _id _value?), typedExpressionIR_arg

 .. Case analysis on actctxt
  ... Case `actctxt` matches pattern `ACTION`
   .... Let `typedExpressionIR?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR_arg, typeIR_param)`
   .... Assert that typedExpressionIR? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?(typedExpressionIR_arg_cast)` be typedExpressionIR?
   .... Result in typedExpressionIR_arg_cast

  ... Case `actctxt` matches pattern `NOACTION`
   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_arg ctk_arg )))` be typedExpressionIR_arg
   .... If [`typeIR_param` and `typeIR_arg` are the same type](Type_alpha), then
    ..... Assert that ~ctk_arg matches pattern `DYN`
    ..... Result in typedExpressionIR_arg

relation Call_convention_argument_ok: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR, argumentIR

. Assert that `argumentIR` has type typedExpressionIR
. Let `typedExpressionIR` be `argumentIR` as typedExpressionIR
. Group typedExpression: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR, `typedExpressionIR` as argumentIR

(Call_convention_expr_ok: p TC actctxt |- parameterTypeIR @ typedExpressionIR : typedExpressionIR_cast)
 .. Result in `typedExpressionIR_cast` as argumentIR
. Case analysis on argumentIR
 .. Case `argumentIR` matches pattern `%=%`
  ... Let `(nameIR = typedExpressionIR)` be argumentIR
  ... Group nameIR-typedExpression: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR, (nameIR = typedExpressionIR)

(Call_convention_expr_ok: p TC actctxt |- parameterTypeIR @ typedExpressionIR : typedExpressionIR_cast)
   .... Result in (nameIR = typedExpressionIR_cast)

 .. Case `argumentIR` matches pattern `%=_`
  ... Let `(nameIR =_)` be argumentIR
  ... Group nameIR-dontcare: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR, (nameIR =_)

   .... Let `(direction _typeIR _id _value?)` be parameterTypeIR
   .... Assert that direction matches pattern `OUT`
   .... Result in (nameIR =_)

 .. Case `argumentIR` matches pattern `_`
  ... Group dontcare: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR, (_)

   .... Let `(direction _typeIR _id _value?)` be parameterTypeIR
   .... Assert that direction matches pattern `OUT`
   .... Result in (_)

relation Call_convention_ok: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR*, argumentIR*

. Case analysis on parameterTypeIR*
 .. Case `parameterTypeIR*` matches pattern []
  ... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p, TC, actctxt, [], []

   .... Result in []

 .. Case `parameterTypeIR*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `parameterTypeIR_h :: parameterTypeIR_t*` be parameterTypeIR*
  ... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `argumentIR_h :: argumentIR_t*` be argumentIR*
  ... Group cons: p, TC, actctxt, parameterTypeIR_h :: parameterTypeIR_t*, argumentIR_h :: argumentIR_t*

(Call_convention_argument_ok: p TC actctxt |- parameterTypeIR_h @ argumentIR_h : argumentIR_h_cast)
(Call_convention_ok: p TC actctxt |- parameterTypeIR_t* @ argumentIR_t* : argumentIR_t_cast*)
   .... Result in argumentIR_h_cast :: argumentIR_t_cast*

relation RoutineTarget_ok: p, TC, routineTarget

. Case analysis on routineTarget
 .. Case `routineTarget` has type prefixedNonTypeName
  ... Let `prefixedNonTypeName` be `routineTarget` as prefixedNonTypeName
  ... Group prefixedNonTypeName: p, TC, `prefixedNonTypeName` as routineTarget

   .... Let `prefixedNameIR` be `$prefixedNonTypeName(prefixedNonTypeName)`
   .... Assert that (prefixedNameIR = (` "verify")) \/ (prefixedNameIR = (. "verify")) => (p = (block)) /\ `$is_parser_blockKind(TC.block.kind)` \/ (p = (local)) /\ `$is_parser_state_localKind(TC.local.kind)`
   .... Result in `prefixedNameIR` as routineTargetIR

 .. Case `routineTarget` has type memberAccessExpression
  ... Let `(memberAccessBase . member)` be `routineTarget` as memberAccessExpression
  ... Case analysis on memberAccessBase
   .... Case `memberAccessBase` has type prefixedTypeName
    ..... Let `prefixedTypeName` be `memberAccessBase` as prefixedTypeName
    ..... Group memberAccessExpression-prefixedTypeName: p, TC, `(`prefixedTypeName` as memberAccessBase . member)` as routineTarget

     ...... Let `prefixedNameIR` be `$prefixedTypeName(prefixedTypeName)`
     ...... Let `nameIR` be `$name(member)`
     ...... Result in (type prefixedNameIR . nameIR)

   .... Case `memberAccessBase` has type expression
    ..... Let `expression_base` be `memberAccessBase` as expression
    ..... Group memberAccessExpression-expression: p, TC, `(`expression_base` as memberAccessBase . member)` as routineTarget

     ...... Let `nameIR` be `$name(member)`
     ...... Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression_base`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](Expr_ok)
     ...... Result in (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR)

 .. Case `routineTarget` has type parenthesizedExpression
  ... Let `(( expression ))` be `routineTarget` as parenthesizedExpression
  ... Group parenthesizedExpression: p, TC, `(( expression ))` as routineTarget

   .... Let `RoutineTarget_ok` be [`routineTargetIR` be the result of typing `expression`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](RoutineTarget_ok)
   .... Result in (( routineTargetIR ))
. Assert that `routineTarget` has type referenceExpression
. Let `referenceExpression` be `routineTarget` as referenceExpression
. Assert that referenceExpression matches pattern `THIS`
. Group this: p, TC, `(this)` as routineTarget

 .. Result in `(` "this")` as routineTargetIR

relation RoutineTarget_lvalue_ok: p, TC, lvalue

. Group : p, TC, lvalue

 .. Let `expression` be `$lvalue(lvalue)`
 .. Let `RoutineTarget_ok` be [`routineTargetIR` be the result of typing `expression`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC`](RoutineTarget_ok)
 .. Result in routineTargetIR

relation RoutineType_ok: p, TC, routineTargetIR'', typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR''*

. Assert that `routineTargetIR''` has type prefixedNameIR
. Let `prefixedNameIR` be `routineTargetIR''` as prefixedNameIR
. Group referenceExpressionIR: p, TC, `prefixedNameIR` as routineTargetIR, typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR*

 .. Let `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)?` be `$find_routine_overloaded(p, TC, prefixedNameIR, argumentIR''*)`
 .. Assert that (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?((rid, routineTypeDefIR, id_default*))` be (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)?
 .. Let `(routineTypeDefIR, tid*)` be `$specialize_routineTypeDef(routineTypeDefIR, typeArgumentIR*)`
 .. Assert that `(routineTypeDefIR, tid*)` has type (functionTypeIR, tid*)
 .. Let `(functionTypeIR, tid_impl*)` be `(routineTypeDefIR, tid*)` as (functionTypeIR, tid*)
 .. Let `bound` be `$union_set<tid>(`$bound(p, TC)`, ({ tid_impl* }))`
 .. If [``functionTypeIR` as routineTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](RoutineType_wf), then
  ... Result in `functionTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, tid_impl*, id_default*
. Case analysis on routineTargetIR''
 .. Case `routineTargetIR''` matches pattern `%.%`
  ... Let `(typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR)` be routineTargetIR''
  ... Assert that typeArgumentIR* matches pattern []
  ... Case analysis on argumentIR''*
   .... Case `argumentIR''*` matches pattern []
    ..... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-builtin-method-minmax-SizeIn-BitsBytes: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR), [], []

     ...... Assert that nameIR is in ["minSizeInBits", "minSizeInBytes", "maxSizeInBits", "maxSizeInBytes"]
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR` be (builtin_method( [] )-> `(int)` as typeIR)
     ...... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], []
    ..... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-builtin-method-header-isValid: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR), [], []

     ...... Assert that nameIR is in ["isValid"]
     ...... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
     ...... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
     ...... Assert that `typeIR` has type headerTypeIR
     ...... Let `(header _tid { _fieldTypeIR* })` be `typeIR` as headerTypeIR
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR` be (builtin_method( [] )-> `(bool)` as typeIR)
     ...... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], []
    ..... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-builtin-method-union-isValid: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR), [], []

     ...... Assert that nameIR is in ["isValid"]
     ...... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
     ...... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
     ...... Assert that `typeIR` has type headerUnionTypeIR
     ...... Let `(header_union _tid { _fieldTypeIR* })` be `typeIR` as headerUnionTypeIR
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR` be (builtin_method( [] )-> `(bool)` as typeIR)
     ...... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], []
    ..... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-builtin-method-header-set-ValidInvalid: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR), [], []

     ...... Assert that nameIR is in ["setValid", "setInvalid"]
     ...... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
     ...... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
     ...... Assert that `typeIR` has type headerTypeIR
     ...... Let `(header _tid { _fieldTypeIR* })` be `typeIR` as headerTypeIR
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR` be (builtin_method( [] )-> `(void)` as typeIR)
     ...... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], []

   .... Case `argumentIR''*` matches pattern [ _/1 ]
    ..... Let `[argumentIR]` be argumentIR''*
    ..... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-builtin-method-stack-push-pop-front: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR), [], [argumentIR]

     ...... Assert that nameIR is in ["push_front", "pop_front"]
     ...... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
     ...... Let `typeIR'` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
     ...... Assert that `typeIR'` has type headerStackTypeIR
     ...... Let `(typeIR [ _nat ])` be `typeIR'` as headerStackTypeIR
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR` be (() `(int)` as typeIR "count" ?())
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR` be (builtin_method( [parameterTypeIR] )-> `(int)` as typeIR)
     ...... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], []
  ... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-extern-method: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR), typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR*

   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
   .... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
   .... Assert that `typeIR` has type externObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `(extern _tid ({ (rid_f : routineTypeDefIR_f)* }))` be `typeIR` as externObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `id_arg?*` be `$ids_arguments(argumentIR''*)`
   .... Let `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)?` be `$find_overloaded<routineTypeDefIR>(({ (rid_f : routineTypeDefIR_f)* }), nameIR, id_arg?*)`
   .... Assert that (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?((rid, routineTypeDefIR, id_default*))` be (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)?
   .... Let `(routineTypeDefIR, tid*)` be `$specialize_routineTypeDef(routineTypeDefIR, typeArgumentIR*)`
   .... Assert that `(routineTypeDefIR, tid*)` has type (routineTypeIR, tid*)
   .... Let `(routineTypeIR, tid_impl*)` be `(routineTypeDefIR, tid*)` as (routineTypeIR, tid*)
   .... Let `bound` be `$union_set<tid>(`$bound(p, TC)`, ({ tid_impl* }))`
   .... If [`routineTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](RoutineType_wf), then
    ..... Result in routineTypeIR, tid_impl*, id_default*
  ... Assert that (nameIR = "apply")
  ... Assert that typeArgumentIR* matches pattern []
  ... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-parser-apply-method: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . "apply"), [], argumentIR*

   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
   .... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
   .... Assert that `typeIR` has type parserObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `(parser( parameterTypeIR* ))` be `typeIR` as parserObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (parser_apply( parameterTypeIR* ))
   .... Let `_direction*` be the list of `_direction` and `_typeIR*` be the list of `_typeIR` and `id_param*` be the list of `id_param` and `value_param?*` be the list of `value_param?`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `(_direction _typeIR id_param value_param?)` be parameterTypeIR
   .... for each `parameterTypeIR` in `parameterTypeIR*`
   .... Let `pid*` be the list of `pid`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `pid` be (id_param # (value_param? =/= ?()))
   .... for each `id_param` in `id_param*` and `value_param?` in `value_param?*`
   .... Let `rid` be ("apply" ( pid* ))
   .... Let `id_arg?*` be `$ids_arguments(argumentIR''*)`
   .... Let `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)'?` be `$find_overloaded<routineTypeDefIR>(({ [(rid : `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeDefIR)] }), "apply", id_arg?*)`
   .... Assert that (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)'? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?((rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*))` be (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)'?
   .... Assert that `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)` has type (rid, methodTypeIR, id*)
   .... Let `(rid', methodTypeIR', id_default*)` be `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)` as (rid, methodTypeIR, id*)
   .... Assert that (rid' = rid)
   .... Assert that (methodTypeIR' = methodTypeIR)
   .... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], id_default*
  ... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-control-apply-method: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . "apply"), [], argumentIR*

   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
   .... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
   .... Assert that `typeIR` has type controlObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `(control( parameterTypeIR* ))` be `typeIR` as controlObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (control_apply( parameterTypeIR* ))
   .... Let `_direction*` be the list of `_direction` and `_typeIR*` be the list of `_typeIR` and `id_param*` be the list of `id_param` and `value_param?*` be the list of `value_param?`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `(_direction _typeIR id_param value_param?)` be parameterTypeIR
   .... for each `parameterTypeIR` in `parameterTypeIR*`
   .... Let `pid*` be the list of `pid`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `pid` be (id_param # (value_param? =/= ?()))
   .... for each `id_param` in `id_param*` and `value_param?` in `value_param?*`
   .... Let `rid` be ("apply" ( pid* ))
   .... Let `id_arg?*` be `$ids_arguments(argumentIR''*)`
   .... Let `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)'?` be `$find_overloaded<routineTypeDefIR>(({ [(rid : `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeDefIR)] }), "apply", id_arg?*)`
   .... Assert that (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)'? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?((rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*))` be (rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)'?
   .... Assert that `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)` has type (rid, methodTypeIR, id*)
   .... Let `(rid', methodTypeIR', id_default*)` be `(rid, routineTypeDefIR, id*)` as (rid, methodTypeIR, id*)
   .... Assert that (rid' = rid)
   .... Assert that (methodTypeIR' = methodTypeIR)
   .... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], id_default*
  ... Assert that argumentIR''* matches pattern []
  ... Group typedExpressionIR-nameIR-table-apply-method: p, TC, (typedExpressionIR_base . "apply"), [], []

   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
   .... Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
   .... Assert that `typeIR` has type tableObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `(table _tid # typeIR_table)` be `typeIR` as tableObjectTypeIR
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (table_apply-> typeIR_table)
   .... Result in `methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR, [], []

 .. Case `routineTargetIR''` matches pattern `(%)`
  ... Let `(( routineTargetIR ))` be routineTargetIR''
  ... Group parenthesized: p, TC, (( routineTargetIR )), typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR*

(RoutineType_ok: p TC |- routineTargetIR < typeArgumentIR* >( argumentIR''* ): routineTypeIR <# tid_inserted* >(# id_default* ))
   .... Result in routineTypeIR, tid_inserted*, id_default*

relation Call_ok: p, TC, routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid'*, argumentIR*, id_default'*

. Case analysis on routineTypeIR
 .. Case `routineTypeIR` has type functionTypeIR
  ... Let `functionTypeIR` be `routineTypeIR` as functionTypeIR
  ... Case analysis on functionTypeIR
   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `BUILTIN_FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(builtin_function( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that tid'* matches pattern []
    ..... Group builtin-function: p, TC, `(builtin_function( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, [], argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Assert that `$callable_builtin_function(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
     ...... Result in typeIR_ret, typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR_cast*

   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(function( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Group function: p, TC, `(function( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `inference` be `$infer(tid'*, parameterTypeIR_aligned*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `typeIR?*` be the list of `typeIR?`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `typeIR?` be `$find_map<tid, typeIR>(inference, tid')`
     ...... for each `tid'` in `tid'*`
     ...... Assert that typeIR? matches pattern (_), for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_inferred*` be the list of `typeIR_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `?(typeIR_inferred)` be typeIR?
     ...... for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeArgumentIR_inferred*` be typeArgumentIR* ++ typeIR_inferred*
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred` be `$subst_parameterType(inference, parameterTypeIR_aligned)`
     ...... for each `parameterTypeIR_aligned` in `parameterTypeIR_aligned*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_ret_inferred` be `$subst_type(inference, typeIR_ret)`
     ...... Let `functionTypeIR_inferred` be (function( parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* )-> typeIR_ret_inferred)
     ...... If [``functionTypeIR_inferred` as routineTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(p, TC)``](RoutineType_wf), then
      ....... Assert that `$callable_function(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
      ....... Result in typeIR_ret_inferred, typeArgumentIR_inferred*, argumentIR_cast*

   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `EXTERN_FUNCTION(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(extern_function( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Group externfunction: p, TC, `(extern_function( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `inference` be `$infer(tid'*, parameterTypeIR_aligned*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `typeIR?*` be the list of `typeIR?`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `typeIR?` be `$find_map<tid, typeIR>(inference, tid')`
     ...... for each `tid'` in `tid'*`
     ...... Assert that typeIR? matches pattern (_), for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_inferred*` be the list of `typeIR_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `?(typeIR_inferred)` be typeIR?
     ...... for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeArgumentIR_inferred*` be typeArgumentIR* ++ typeIR_inferred*
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred` be `$subst_parameterType(inference, parameterTypeIR_aligned)`
     ...... for each `parameterTypeIR_aligned` in `parameterTypeIR_aligned*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_ret_inferred` be `$subst_type(inference, typeIR_ret)`
     ...... Let `functionTypeIR_inferred` be (extern_function( parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* )-> typeIR_ret_inferred)
     ...... If [``functionTypeIR_inferred` as routineTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(p, TC)``](RoutineType_wf), then
      ....... Assert that `$callable_extern_function(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
      ....... Result in typeIR_ret_inferred, typeArgumentIR_inferred*, argumentIR_cast*

   .... Case `functionTypeIR` matches pattern `ACTION(%)`
    ..... Let `(action( parameterTypeIR* ))` be functionTypeIR
    ..... Assert that typeArgumentIR* matches pattern []
    ..... Assert that tid'* matches pattern []
    ..... Group action: p, TC, `(action( parameterTypeIR* ))` as routineTypeIR, [], [], argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Assert that `$callable_action(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (action) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
     ...... Result in `(void)` as typeIR, [], argumentIR_cast*

 .. Case `routineTypeIR` has type methodTypeIR
  ... Let `methodTypeIR` be `routineTypeIR` as methodTypeIR
  ... Case analysis on methodTypeIR
   .... Case `methodTypeIR` matches pattern `BUILTIN_METHOD(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(builtin_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be methodTypeIR
    ..... Assert that tid'* matches pattern []
    ..... Group builtin-method: p, TC, `(builtin_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, [], argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Assert that `$callable_builtin_method(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
     ...... Result in typeIR_ret, typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR_cast*

   .... Case `methodTypeIR` matches pattern `EXTERN_METHOD(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(extern_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be methodTypeIR
    ..... Group extern-method: p, TC, `(extern_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `inference` be `$infer(tid'*, parameterTypeIR_aligned*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `typeIR?*` be the list of `typeIR?`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `typeIR?` be `$find_map<tid, typeIR>(inference, tid')`
     ...... for each `tid'` in `tid'*`
     ...... Assert that typeIR? matches pattern (_), for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_inferred*` be the list of `typeIR_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `?(typeIR_inferred)` be typeIR?
     ...... for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeArgumentIR_inferred*` be typeArgumentIR* ++ typeIR_inferred*
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred` be `$subst_parameterType(inference, parameterTypeIR_aligned)`
     ...... for each `parameterTypeIR_aligned` in `parameterTypeIR_aligned*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_ret_inferred` be `$subst_type(inference, typeIR_ret)`
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR_inferred` be (extern_method( parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* )-> typeIR_ret_inferred)
     ...... If [``methodTypeIR_inferred` as routineTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(p, TC)``](RoutineType_wf), then
      ....... Assert that `$callable_extern_method(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
      ....... Result in typeIR_ret_inferred, typeArgumentIR_inferred*, argumentIR_cast*

   .... Case `methodTypeIR` matches pattern `EXTERN_METHODABSTRACT(%)->%`
    ..... Let `(extern_methodabstract( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` be methodTypeIR
    ..... Group extern-abstract-method: p, TC, `(extern_methodabstract( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)` as routineTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default'*)`
     ...... Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `inference` be `$infer(tid'*, parameterTypeIR_aligned*, argumentIR*)`
     ...... Let `typeIR?*` be the list of `typeIR?`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `typeIR?` be `$find_map<tid, typeIR>(inference, tid')`
     ...... for each `tid'` in `tid'*`
     ...... Assert that typeIR? matches pattern (_), for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_inferred*` be the list of `typeIR_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `?(typeIR_inferred)` be typeIR?
     ...... for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
     ...... Let `typeArgumentIR_inferred*` be typeArgumentIR* ++ typeIR_inferred*
     ...... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
      ....... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred` be `$subst_parameterType(inference, parameterTypeIR_aligned)`
     ...... for each `parameterTypeIR_aligned` in `parameterTypeIR_aligned*`
     ...... Let `typeIR_ret_inferred` be `$subst_type(inference, typeIR_ret)`
     ...... Let `methodTypeIR_inferred` be (extern_methodabstract( parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* )-> typeIR_ret_inferred)
     ...... If [``methodTypeIR_inferred` as routineTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(p, TC)``](RoutineType_wf), then
      ....... Assert that `$callable_extern_abstract_method(p, TC)`
(Call_convention_ok: p TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
      ....... Result in typeIR_ret_inferred, typeArgumentIR_inferred*, argumentIR_cast*

   .... Case `methodTypeIR` matches pattern `PARSER_APPLY(%)`
    ..... Let `(parser_apply( parameterTypeIR* ))` be methodTypeIR
