    ..... Assert that tid'* matches pattern []

relation ConstructorType_ok: p, TC, prefixedNameIR, typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR*

. Group : p, TC, prefixedNameIR, typeArgumentIR*, argumentIR*

 .. Let `(rid, constructorTypeDefIR, id*)?` be `$find_constructor_overloaded(TC, prefixedNameIR, argumentIR*)`
 .. Assert that (rid, constructorTypeDefIR, id*)? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?((cid, constructorTypeDefIR, id_default*))` be (rid, constructorTypeDefIR, id*)?
 .. Let `(constructorTypeIR, tid_impl*)` be `$specialize_constructorTypeDef(constructorTypeDefIR, typeArgumentIR*)`
 .. Let `bound` be `$union_set<tid>(`$bound(p, TC)`, ({ tid_impl* }))`
 .. If [`constructorTypeIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids `bound`](ConstructorType_wf), then
  ... Result in constructorTypeIR, tid_impl*, id_default*

relation Inst_ok: cursor', TC, instctxt, constructorTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

. Assert that cursor' matches pattern `GLOBAL`
. Group package: (global), TC, instctxt, constructorTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

 .. Let `(constructor( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_object)` be constructorTypeIR
 .. Let `typeIR` be `$canon(typeIR_object)`
 .. Assert that `typeIR` has type packageObjectTypeIR
 .. Let `(package< _typeIR* >)` be `typeIR` as packageObjectTypeIR
 .. Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default*)`
 .. Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
 .. Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
 .. Let `inference` be `$infer(tid_infer*, parameterTypeIR_aligned*, argumentIR*)`
 .. Let `typeIR'?*` be the list of `typeIR'?`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `typeIR'?` be `$find_map<tid, typeIR>(inference, tid_infer)`
 .. for each `tid_infer` in `tid_infer*`
 .. Assert that typeIR'? matches pattern (_), for each `typeIR'?` in `typeIR'?*`
 .. Let `typeIR_inferred*` be the list of `typeIR_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `?(typeIR_inferred)` be typeIR'?
 .. for each `typeIR'?` in `typeIR'?*`
 .. Let `typeArgumentIR_inferred*` be typeArgumentIR* ++ typeIR_inferred*
 .. Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred` be `$subst_parameterType(inference, parameterTypeIR_aligned)`
 .. for each `parameterTypeIR_aligned` in `parameterTypeIR_aligned*`
 .. Let `typeIR_object_inferred` be `$subst_type(inference, typeIR_object)`
 .. Let `constructorTypeIR_inferred` be (constructor( parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* )-> typeIR_object_inferred)
 .. If [`constructorTypeIR_inferred` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound((global), TC)``](ConstructorType_wf), then
  ... Assert that `$instantiable((global), TC, instctxt, typeIR_object_inferred)`
(Call_convention_ok: (block) TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
  ... Result in typeIR_object_inferred, typeArgumentIR_inferred*, argumentIR_cast*
. Group non-package: p, TC, instctxt, constructorTypeIR, typeArgumentIR*, tid_infer*, argumentIR*, id_default*

 .. Let `(constructor( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_object)` be constructorTypeIR
 .. Assert that ~`$is_package_object_typeIR(`$canon(typeIR_object)`)`
 .. Let `parameterTypeIR_non_default*` be `$filter_default_parameters(parameterTypeIR*, id_default*)`
 .. Assert that (|parameterTypeIR_non_default*| = |argumentIR*|)
 .. Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned*` be `$align_parameters(parameterTypeIR_non_default*, argumentIR*)`
 .. Let `inference` be `$infer(tid_infer*, parameterTypeIR_aligned*, argumentIR*)`
 .. Let `typeIR?*` be the list of `typeIR?`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `typeIR?` be `$find_map<tid, typeIR>(inference, tid_infer)`
 .. for each `tid_infer` in `tid_infer*`
 .. Assert that typeIR? matches pattern (_), for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
 .. Let `typeIR_inferred*` be the list of `typeIR_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `?(typeIR_inferred)` be typeIR?
 .. for each `typeIR?` in `typeIR?*`
 .. Let `typeArgumentIR_inferred*` be typeArgumentIR* ++ typeIR_inferred*
 .. Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred`, obtained by repeating:
  ... Let `parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred` be `$subst_parameterType(inference, parameterTypeIR_aligned)`
 .. for each `parameterTypeIR_aligned` in `parameterTypeIR_aligned*`
 .. Let `typeIR_object_inferred` be `$subst_type(inference, typeIR_object)`
 .. Let `constructorTypeIR_inferred` be (constructor( parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* )-> typeIR_object_inferred)
 .. If [`constructorTypeIR_inferred` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(cursor', TC)``](ConstructorType_wf), then
  ... Assert that `$instantiable(cursor', TC, instctxt, typeIR_object_inferred)`
(Call_convention_ok: cursor' TC (noaction) |- parameterTypeIR_aligned_inferred* @ argumentIR* : argumentIR_cast*)
  ... Result in typeIR_object_inferred, typeArgumentIR_inferred*, argumentIR_cast*

relation SwitchLabel_table_ok: TC, tid_table, bool', switchLabel

. Assert that (bool' = true)
. Assert that switchLabel matches pattern `DEFAULT`
. Group default: TC, tid_table, true, (default)

 .. Result in (default)
. Assert that `switchLabel` has type prefixedNonTypeName
. Let `prefixedNonTypeName` be `switchLabel` as prefixedNonTypeName
. Group expressionNonBrace-prefixedNonTypeName: TC, tid_table, _bool, `prefixedNonTypeName` as switchLabel

 .. Let `prefixedNameIR` be `$prefixedNonTypeName(prefixedNonTypeName)`
 .. Assert that prefixedNameIR matches pattern ``%`
 .. Let `(` nameIR_label)` be prefixedNameIR
 .. Let `tid_table_enum` be "action_list(" ++ tid_table ++ ")"
 .. Let `id_label` be tid_table_enum ++ "." ++ nameIR_label
 .. Let `varTypeIR?` be `$find_var((local), TC, (` id_label))`
 .. Assert that varTypeIR? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?((_direction typeIR_label ctk_label value?))` be varTypeIR?
 .. Assert that value? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?(value_label)` be value?
 .. Assert that (value_label = `(table_enum tid_table_enum . nameIR_label)` as value)
 .. Let `typedExpressionIR_label` be (`(` nameIR_label)` as expressionIR # (( typeIR_label ctk_label )))
 .. Result in `typedExpressionIR_label` as switchLabelIR

(SwitchLabel_table_ok: TC tid_table b_last |- switchLabel : switchLabelIR)

relation SwitchLabel_general_ok: TC, _typeIR', bool', switchLabel

 .. Assert that typedExpressionIR? matches pattern (_)

 .. Let `(_expressionIR # (( _typeIR ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_label_cast
 .. Assert that ctk matches pattern `LCTK`

relation Decl_object_ok: p, TC_0, frame, rdenv, objectDeclaration

. Case analysis on objectDeclaration
 .. Case `objectDeclaration` has type functionDeclaration
  ... Let `(annotationList (typeOrVoid name typeParameterListOpt ( parameterList )) blockStatement)` be `objectDeclaration` as functionDeclaration
  ... Group functionDeclaration: p, TC_0, frame, rdenv, `(annotationList (typeOrVoid name typeParameterListOpt ( parameterList )) blockStatement)` as objectDeclaration

   .... Let `TC_1` be TC_0[block.kind = (extern)]
   .... Let `TC_2` be TC_1[block.frame = frame]
   .... Let `typeParameter*` be `$flatten_typeParameterListOpt(typeParameterListOpt)`
   .... Let `tid_expl*` be the list of `tid_expl`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `tid_expl` be `$name(typeParameter)`
   .... for each `typeParameter` in `typeParameter*`
   .... Let `TC_3` be `$add_types((local), TC_2, tid_expl*, `(tid tid_expl)` as typeDefIR*)`
   .... Let `Type_ok` be [the result of typing `typeOrVoid`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_3`, 
and the set of inserted type variables, respectively](Type_ok)
   .... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
   .... Let `parameter*` be `$flatten_parameterList(parameterList)`
   .... Let `Parameters_ok` be [`parameterIR*` be the result of typing `parameter*`, under cursor `(local)` and typing context `TC_3`](Parameters_ok)
   .... Let `parameterTypeIR*` be the list of `parameterTypeIR`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `parameterTypeIR` be `$parameterIR(parameterIR)`
   .... for each `parameterIR` in `parameterIR*`
   .... Let `TC_4` be `$add_parameters((local), TC_3, parameterTypeIR*)`
   .... Let `TC_5` be TC_4[local.kind = (extern_method-> typeIR_ret)]
(Block_ok: TC_5 (cont) |- blockStatement : _typingContext f blockStatementIR)
   .... Assert that f matches pattern `RET`
   .... Let `rid` be `$rid(name, parameterList)`
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (extern_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR` be `(`methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >)` as routineTypeDefIR
   .... If [`routineTypeDefIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(p, TC_0)``](RoutineTypeDef_wf), then
    ..... Let `rdenv_init` be `$update_map<rid, routineTypeDefIR>(rdenv, rid, routineTypeDefIR)`
    ..... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
    ..... Let `functionDeclarationIR` be (annotationList (typeIR_ret nameIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >( parameterIR* )) blockStatementIR)
    ..... Result in frame, rdenv_init, `functionDeclarationIR` as objectDeclarationIR
   .... Assert that (typeIR_ret = `(void)` as typeIR)
   .... Let `rid` be `$rid(name, parameterList)`
   .... Let `methodTypeIR` be (extern_method( parameterTypeIR* )-> typeIR_ret)
   .... Let `routineTypeDefIR` be `(`methodTypeIR` as routineTypeIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >)` as routineTypeDefIR
   .... If [`routineTypeDefIR` is a well-formed type, assuming bound type ids ``$bound(p, TC_0)``](RoutineTypeDef_wf), then
    ..... Let `rdenv_init` be `$update_map<rid, routineTypeDefIR>(rdenv, rid, routineTypeDefIR)`
    ..... Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
    ..... Let `functionDeclarationIR` be (annotationList (typeIR_ret nameIR < tid_expl* , tid_impl* >( parameterIR* )) blockStatementIR)
    ..... Result in frame, rdenv_init, `functionDeclarationIR` as objectDeclarationIR

 .. Case `objectDeclaration` has type instantiation
  ... Let `instantiation` be `objectDeclaration` as instantiation
  ... Group instantiation: p, TC_0, frame, rdenv, `instantiation` as objectDeclaration

   .... Let `Decl_ok` be [the result of typing ``instantiation` as declaration`, under cursor `p` and typing context `TC_0`,
and the modified typing context, respectively](Decl_ok)
   .... Assert that `declarationIR` has type instantiationIR
   .... Let `instantiationIR` be `declarationIR` as instantiationIR
   .... Let `(_annotationList _typeIR _prefixedNameIR < _typeArgumentListIR >( _argumentListIR ) nameIR _objectInitializerOptIR ;)` be instantiationIR
   .... Let `varTypeIR'?` be `$find_var(p, TC_1, (` nameIR))`
   .... Assert that varTypeIR'? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?(varTypeIR)` be varTypeIR'?
   .... Let `frame_init` be `$update_map<id, varTypeIR>(frame, nameIR, varTypeIR)`
   .... Result in frame_init, rdenv, `instantiationIR` as objectDeclarationIR

relation Decls_object_ok: p, TC, frame', rdenv', objectDeclaration*

. Case analysis on objectDeclaration*
 .. Case `objectDeclaration*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p, TC, frame, rdenv, []

   .... Result in frame', rdenv', []

 .. Case `objectDeclaration*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `objectDeclaration_h :: objectDeclaration_t*` be objectDeclaration*
  ... Group cons: p, TC, frame_0, rdenv_0, objectDeclaration_h :: objectDeclaration_t*

(Decl_object_ok: p TC frame' rdenv' |- objectDeclaration_h : frame_1 rdenv_1 objectDeclarationIR_h)
(Decls_object_ok: p TC frame_1 rdenv_1 |- objectDeclaration_t* : frame_2 rdenv_2 objectDeclarationIR_t*)
   .... Result in frame_2, rdenv_2, objectDeclarationIR_h :: objectDeclarationIR_t*

relation Enum_serializable_field_ok: TC_0, nameIR_enum, typeIR, (name = expression)

. Group : TC_0, nameIR_enum, typeIR, (name = expression)

 .. Let `Expr_ok` be [the result of typing `expression`, under cursor `(block)` and typing context `TC_0`](Expr_ok)
 .. Let `typedExpressionIR'?` be `$coerce_unary(typedExpressionIR, typeIR)`
 .. Assert that typedExpressionIR'? matches pattern (_)
 .. Let `?(typedExpressionIR_cast)` be typedExpressionIR'?
 .. Let `(_expressionIR # (( _typeIR ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_cast
 .. Assert that ctk matches pattern `LCTK`
 .. Let `Eval_static` be [the static evaluation of `typedExpressionIR_cast`, under cursor `(block)` and typing context `TC_0`](Eval_static)
 .. Let `nameIR` be `$name(name)`
 .. Let `typeIR_enum` be `(enum nameIR_enum # typeIR { [] })` as typeIR
 .. Let `value_enum` be `(nameIR_enum . nameIR # value)` as value
 .. Let `TC_1` be `$add_var((block), TC_0, nameIR, (() typeIR_enum (lctk) ?(value_enum)))`
 .. Result in TC_1, (nameIR = typedExpressionIR_cast), value

relation Enum_serializable_fields_ok: TC', nameIR_enum, typeIR, namedExpression*

. Case analysis on namedExpression*
 .. Case `namedExpression*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: TC, nameIR_enum, typeIR, []

   .... Result in TC', [], []

      ....... The relation holds

relation Expr_inst: p, IC, STO', (expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR')

. Case analysis on expressionIR
 .. Case `expressionIR` has type booleanLiteral
  ... Let `booleanLiteral` be `expressionIR` as booleanLiteral
  ... Group literalExpressionIR-boolean: p, IC, STO, (`booleanLiteral` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Case analysis on booleanLiteral
    ..... Case `booleanLiteral` matches pattern `TRUE`
     ...... Result in STO', `(b true)` as value

    ..... Case `booleanLiteral` matches pattern `FALSE`
     ...... Result in STO', `(b false)` as value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type numberLiteral
  ... Let `numberLiteral` be `expressionIR` as numberLiteral
  ... Group literalExpression-number: p, IC, STO, (`numberLiteral` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Case analysis on numberLiteral
    ..... Case `numberLiteral` matches pattern `D%`
     ...... Let `(d i)` be numberLiteral
     ...... Result in STO', `(d i)` as value

    ..... Case `numberLiteral` matches pattern `%W%`
     ...... Let `(n w i)` be numberLiteral
     ...... Result in STO', `(n w i)` as value

    ..... Case `numberLiteral` matches pattern `%S%`
     ...... Let `(n s i)` be numberLiteral
     ...... Result in STO', `(n s i)` as value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type stringLiteral
  ... Let `(" text ")` be `expressionIR` as stringLiteral
  ... Group literalExpressionIR-stringLiteral: p, IC, STO, (`(" text ")` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Result in STO', `(" text ")` as value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type prefixedNameIR
  ... Let `prefixedNameIR` be `expressionIR` as prefixedNameIR
  ... Group referenceExpressionIR: p, IC, STO, (`prefixedNameIR` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Let `value` be `$find_value_inst(p, IC, prefixedNameIR)`
   .... Result in STO', value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type defaultExpression
  ... Let `defaultExpression` be `expressionIR` as defaultExpression
  ... Group defaultExpressionIR: p, IC, STO, (`(...)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Result in STO', `(default)` as value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type unaryExpressionIR
  ... Let `(unop typedExpressionIR)` be `expressionIR` as unaryExpressionIR
  ... Group unaryExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, (`(unop typedExpressionIR)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Case analysis on unop
    ..... Case `unop` matches pattern `~`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `$un_bnot(value)`

    ..... Case `unop` matches pattern `!`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `$un_lnot(value)`

    ..... Case `unop` matches pattern `+`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `$un_plus(value)`

    ..... Case `unop` matches pattern `-`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `$un_minus(value)`

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type binaryExpressionIR
  ... Let `(typedExpressionIR_l binop typedExpressionIR_r)` be `expressionIR` as binaryExpressionIR
  ... Group binaryExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, (`(typedExpressionIR_l binop typedExpressionIR_r)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Case analysis on binop
    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `+`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_plus(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `|+|`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_satplus(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `-`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_minus(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `|-|`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_satminus(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `*`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_mul(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `/`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_div(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `%`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_mod(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `<<`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_shl(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `>>`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_shr(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `<=`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `(b `$bin_le(value_l, value_r)`)` as value

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `>=`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `(b `$bin_ge(value_l, value_r)`)` as value

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `<`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `(b `$bin_lt(value_l, value_r)`)` as value

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `>`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `(b `$bin_gt(value_l, value_r)`)` as value

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `==`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `(b `$bin_eq(value_l, value_r)`)` as value

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `!=`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `(b `$bin_ne(value_l, value_r)`)` as value

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `&`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_band(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `^`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_bxor(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `|`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_bor(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `++`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_concat(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `&&`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_land(value_l, value_r)`

    ..... Case `binop` matches pattern `||`
(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_l ==> STO_1 value_l)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_r ==> STO_2 value_r)
     ...... Result in STO_2, `$bin_lor(value_l, value_r)`

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type ternaryExpressionIR
  ... Let `(typedExpressionIR_cond ? typedExpressionIR_true : typedExpressionIR_false)` be `expressionIR` as ternaryExpressionIR
  ... Group ternaryExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, (`(typedExpressionIR_cond ? typedExpressionIR_true : typedExpressionIR_false)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_cond ==> STO_1 value)
   .... Assert that (value = `(b true)` as value)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_true ==> STO_2 value_true)
   .... Result in STO_2, value_true
   .... Assert that (value = `(b false)` as value)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_false ==> STO_2 value_false)
   .... Result in STO_2, value_false

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type castExpressionIR
  ... Let `(( typeIR ) typedExpressionIR)` be `expressionIR` as castExpressionIR
  ... Group castExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, (`(( typeIR ) typedExpressionIR)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
   .... Let `value_cast` be `$cast_op(typeIR, value)`
   .... Result in STO_1, value_cast

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type dataExpressionIR
  ... Let `dataExpressionIR` be `expressionIR` as dataExpressionIR
  ... Assert that dataExpressionIR matches pattern `{#}`
  ... Group dataExpressionIR-invalid: p, IC, STO, (`({#})` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Result in STO', `({#})` as value
  ... Group dataExpressionIR-sequence: p, IC, STO_0, (`dataExpressionIR` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Case analysis on dataExpressionIR
    ..... Case `dataExpressionIR` matches pattern `SEQ{%}`
     ...... Let `(seq{ typedExpressionListIR })` be dataExpressionIR
(Exprs_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionListIR ==> STO_1 value*)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `(seq( value* ))` as value

    ..... Case `dataExpressionIR` matches pattern `SEQ{%,...}`
     ...... Let `(seq{ typedExpressionListIR ,...})` be dataExpressionIR
(Exprs_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionListIR ==> STO_1 value*)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `(seq( value* ,...))` as value
  ... Group dataExpressionIR-record: p, IC, STO_0, (`dataExpressionIR` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Case analysis on dataExpressionIR
    ..... Case `dataExpressionIR` matches pattern `RECORD{%}`
     ...... Let `(record{ (nameIR = typedExpressionIR)* })` be dataExpressionIR
(Exprs_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR* ==> STO_1 value*)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `(record{ (value nameIR ;)* })` as value

    ..... Case `dataExpressionIR` matches pattern `RECORD{%,...}`
     ...... Let `(record{ (nameIR = typedExpressionIR)* ,...})` be dataExpressionIR
(Exprs_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR* ==> STO_1 value*)
     ...... Result in STO_1, `(record{ (value nameIR ;)* ,...})` as value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type errorAccessExpressionIR
  ... Let `(error. nameIR)` be `expressionIR` as errorAccessExpressionIR
  ... Group accessExpressionIR-errorAccessExpressionIR: p, IC, STO, (`(error. nameIR)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Let `nameIR_error` be "error." ++ nameIR
   .... Let `value_error` be `$find_value_inst(p, IC, (` nameIR_error))`
   .... Result in STO', value_error

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type memberAccessExpressionIR
  ... Let `(memberAccessBaseIR . nameIR)` be `expressionIR` as memberAccessExpressionIR
  ... Assert that memberAccessBaseIR matches pattern `TYPE%`
  ... Let `(type prefixedNameIR)` be memberAccessBaseIR
  ... Group accessExpressionIR-memberAccessExpressionIR-type: p, IC, STO, (`((type prefixedNameIR) . nameIR)` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

   .... Let `typeDefIR'?` be `$find_typedef_inst(p, IC, prefixedNameIR)`
   .... Assert that typeDefIR'? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?(typeDefIR)` be typeDefIR'?
   .... Assert that `typeDefIR` has type enumTypeIR
   .... Let `enumTypeIR` be `typeDefIR` as enumTypeIR
   .... Case analysis on enumTypeIR
    ..... Case `enumTypeIR` matches pattern `ENUM%{%}`
     ...... Let `(enum tid { _id* })` be enumTypeIR
     ...... Result in STO', `(tid . nameIR)` as value

    ..... Case `enumTypeIR` matches pattern `ENUM%#%{%}`
     ...... Let `(enum tid # _typeIR { (id_member = value_member ;)* })` be enumTypeIR
     ...... Let `value'?` be `$assoc_<nameIR, value>(nameIR, (id_member, value_member)*)`
     ...... Assert that value'? matches pattern (_)
     ...... Let `?(value)` be value'?
     ...... Result in STO', `(tid . nameIR # value)` as value
. Let `(( typeIR_base _ctk ))` be _expressionNoteIR'
. Case analysis on expressionIR
 .. Case `expressionIR` has type memberAccessExpressionIR
  ... Let `(memberAccessBaseIR . text)` be `expressionIR` as memberAccessExpressionIR
  ... Assert that `memberAccessBaseIR` has type typedExpressionIR
  ... Let `typedExpressionIR_base` be `memberAccessBaseIR` as typedExpressionIR
  ... Assert that (text = "size")
  ... Group accessExpressionIR-memberAccessExpressionIR-typedExpressionIR-stack-size: p, IC, STO, (`(`typedExpressionIR_base` as memberAccessBaseIR . "size")` as expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))

   .... Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR _ctk' )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
   .... Assert that (typeIR = typeIR_base)
   .... Let `typeIR'` be `$canon(typeIR_base)`
   .... Assert that `typeIR'` has type headerStackTypeIR
   .... Let `(_typeIR [ n_size ])` be `typeIR'` as headerStackTypeIR
   .... Result in STO', `(d `n_size` as int)` as value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type indexAccessExpressionIR
  ... Let `indexAccessExpressionIR` be `expressionIR` as indexAccessExpressionIR
  ... Assert that indexAccessExpressionIR matches pattern `%[%:%]`
  ... Let `(typedExpressionIR_base [ typedExpressionIR_hi : typedExpressionIR_lo ])` be indexAccessExpressionIR
  ... Group indexAccessExpressionIR-bitslice: p, IC, STO_0, (`(typedExpressionIR_base [ typedExpressionIR_hi : typedExpressionIR_lo ])` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_base ==> STO_1 value_base)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_hi ==> STO_2 value_hi)
(Expr_inst: p IC STO_2 |- typedExpressionIR_lo ==> STO_3 value_lo)
   .... Result in STO_3, `$bitacc_op(value_base, value_hi, value_lo)`

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type callExpressionIR
  ... Let `callExpressionIR` be `expressionIR` as callExpressionIR
  ... Case analysis on callExpressionIR
   .... Case `callExpressionIR` matches pattern `%(%)`
    ..... Let `((prefixedNameIR < typeArgumentListIR >) ( argumentListIR ))` be callExpressionIR
    ..... Group callExpressionIR-instantiation: p, IC, STO_0, (`((prefixedNameIR < typeArgumentListIR >) ( argumentListIR ))` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

     ...... Let `(cid, constructor, id*)?` be `$find_constructor_inst(IC, prefixedNameIR, argumentListIR)`
     ...... Assert that (cid, constructor, id*)? matches pattern (_)
     ...... Let `?((_cid, constructor, id_default*))` be (cid, constructor, id*)?
(Constructor_inst: p IC STO' |- constructor < typeArgumentListIR >( argumentListIR # id_default* )==> STO_1 object)
     ...... Let `STO_2` be `$add_map<oid, object>(STO_1, IC.path, object)`
     ...... Let `value_ref` be `(! IC.path)` as value
     ...... Result in STO_2, value_ref

   .... Case `callExpressionIR` matches pattern `%<%>(%)`
    ..... Let `(routineTargetIR < typeArgumentIR* >( argumentIR* ))` be callExpressionIR
    ..... Case analysis on routineTargetIR
     ...... Case `routineTargetIR` matches pattern `%.%`
      ....... Let `(typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR)` be routineTargetIR
      ....... Assert that typeArgumentIR* matches pattern []
      ....... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern []
      ....... Group callExpressionIR-typedeExpressionIR-size: p, IC, STO, (`((typedExpressionIR_base . nameIR) < [] >( [] ))` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

       ........ Let `(_expressionIR # (( typeIR_base _ctk )))` be typedExpressionIR_base
       ........ Assert that nameIR is in ["minSizeInBits", "minSizeInBytes", "maxSizeInBits", "maxSizeInBytes"]
       ........ Result in STO', `$sizeof(typeIR_base, nameIR)`

     ...... Case `routineTargetIR` matches pattern `TYPE%.%`
      ....... Let `(type prefixedNameIR . nameIR)` be routineTargetIR
      ....... Assert that typeArgumentIR* matches pattern []
      ....... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern []
      ....... Group callExpressionIR-type: p, IC, STO, (`((type prefixedNameIR . nameIR) < [] >( [] ))` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

       ........ Let `typeDefIR'?` be `$find_typedef_inst(p, IC, prefixedNameIR)`
       ........ Assert that typeDefIR'? matches pattern (_)
       ........ Let `?(typeDefIR)` be typeDefIR'?
       ........ Case analysis on typeDefIR
        ......... Case `typeDefIR` has type typeIR
         .......... Let `typeIR_base` be `typeDefIR` as typeIR
         .......... Assert that nameIR is in ["minSizeInBits", "minSizeInBytes", "maxSizeInBits", "maxSizeInBytes"]
         .......... Result in STO', `$sizeof(typeIR_base, nameIR)`

        ......... Case `typeDefIR` has type polyTypeDefIR
         .......... Let `(typeIR_base < tid* , tid'* >)` be `typeDefIR` as polyTypeDefIR
         .......... Assert that tid* matches pattern []
         .......... Assert that tid'* matches pattern []
         .......... Assert that nameIR is in ["minSizeInBits", "minSizeInBytes", "maxSizeInBits", "maxSizeInBytes"]
         .......... Result in STO', `$sizeof(typeIR_base, nameIR)`
    ..... Let `(routineTargetIR' < typeArgumentListIR >( argumentListIR ))` be callExpressionIR
    ..... Assert that routineTargetIR' matches pattern `(%)`
    ..... Let `(( routineTargetIR ))` be routineTargetIR'
    ..... Group callExpressionIR-parenthesized: p, IC, STO_0, (`((( routineTargetIR )) < typeArgumentListIR >( argumentListIR ))` as expressionIR # expressionNoteIR)

(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- (`(routineTargetIR < typeArgumentListIR >( argumentListIR ))` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR') ==> STO_1 value)
     ...... Result in STO_1, value

 .. Case `expressionIR` has type parenthesizedExpressionIR
  ... Let `(( typedExpressionIR ))` be `expressionIR` as parenthesizedExpressionIR
  ... Group parenthesizedExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, (`(( typedExpressionIR ))` as expressionIR # _expressionNoteIR)

(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
   .... Result in STO_1, value

relation Exprs_inst: p, IC, STO', typedExpressionIR*

. Case analysis on typedExpressionIR*
 .. Case `typedExpressionIR*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p, IC, STO, []

   .... Result in STO', []

 .. Case `typedExpressionIR*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `typedExpressionIR_h :: typedExpressionIR_t*` be typedExpressionIR*
  ... Group cons: p, IC, STO_0, typedExpressionIR_h :: typedExpressionIR_t*

(Expr_inst: p IC STO' |- typedExpressionIR_h ==> STO_1 value_h)
(Exprs_inst: p IC STO_1 |- typedExpressionIR_t* ==> STO_2 value_t*)
   .... Result in STO_2, value_h :: value_t*

relation Argument_inst: p, IC, STO_0, argumentIR

. Assert that `argumentIR` has type typedExpressionIR
. Let `typedExpressionIR` be `argumentIR` as typedExpressionIR
. Group typedExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, `typedExpressionIR` as argumentIR

(Expr_inst: p IC STO_0 |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
 .. Result in STO_1, value
. Assert that argumentIR matches pattern `%=%`
. Let `(nameIR = typedExpressionIR)` be argumentIR
. Group nameIR-typedExpressionIR: p, IC, STO_0, (nameIR = typedExpressionIR)

(Expr_inst: p IC STO_0 |- typedExpressionIR ==> STO_1 value)
 .. Result in STO_1, value

relation Stmt_inst: p', IC', STO', statementIR

. Case analysis on statementIR
 .. Case `statementIR` has type emptyStatementIR
  ... Let `emptyStatementIR` be `statementIR` as emptyStatementIR
  ... Group emptyStatementIR: p, IC, STO, `emptyStatementIR` as statementIR

   .... Result in IC', STO', `emptyStatementIR` as statementIR

 .. Case `statementIR` has type assignmentStatementIR
  ... Let `assignmentStatementIR` be `statementIR` as assignmentStatementIR
  ... Group assignmentStatementIR: p, IC, STO, `assignmentStatementIR` as statementIR

   .... Result in IC', STO', `assignmentStatementIR` as statementIR

 .. Case `statementIR` has type callStatementIR
  ... Let `callStatementIR` be `statementIR` as callStatementIR
  ... Group callStatementIR: p, IC, STO, `callStatementIR` as statementIR

   .... Result in IC', STO', `callStatementIR` as statementIR

 .. Case `statementIR` has type directApplicationStatementIR
  ... Let `(prefixedNameIR .apply( argumentListIR );)` be `statementIR` as directApplicationStatementIR
  ... Group directApplicationStatementIR: p, IC, STO_0, `(prefixedNameIR .apply( argumentListIR );)` as statementIR

   .... Let `(cid, constructor, id*)?` be `$find_constructor_inst(IC', prefixedNameIR, [])`
   .... Assert that (cid, constructor, id*)? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?((_cid, constructor, _id*))` be (cid, constructor, id*)?
(Constructor_inst: p' IC' STO' |- constructor < [] >( [] # [] )==> STO_1 object)
   .... Let `tid_fresh` be `$fresh_tid`
   .... Let `tid` be `$flatten_prefixedNameIR(prefixedNameIR)`
   .... Let `typeIR` be `(tid tid)` as typeIR
   .... Let `nameIR` be `$concat_text([tid, "_", tid_fresh])`
   .... Let `oid` be IC'.path ++ [nameIR]
   .... Let `STO_2` be `$add_map<oid, object>(STO_1, oid, object)`
   .... Let `constantDeclarationIR` be (() const typeIR nameIR (= `(! oid)` as value) ;)
   .... Let `routineTargetIR` be ((`(` tid)` as expressionIR # (( typeIR (ctk) ))) . "apply")
   .... Let `callStatementIR` be (routineTargetIR < [] >( argumentListIR );)
   .... Let `blockStatementIR` be (() { [`constantDeclarationIR` as blockElementStatementIR, `callStatementIR` as blockElementStatementIR] })
   .... Result in IC', STO_2, `blockStatementIR` as statementIR

 .. Case `statementIR` has type exitStatementIR
  ... Let `exitStatementIR` be `statementIR` as exitStatementIR
  ... Group exitStatementIR: p, IC, STO, `exitStatementIR` as statementIR

   .... Result in IC', STO', `exitStatementIR` as statementIR

 .. Case `statementIR` has type conditionalStatementIR
  ... Let `conditionalStatementIR` be `statementIR` as conditionalStatementIR
  ... Group conditionalStatementIR: p, IC_0, STO_0, `conditionalStatementIR` as statementIR

   .... Case analysis on conditionalStatementIR
    ..... Case `conditionalStatementIR` matches pattern `IF(%)%`
     ...... Let `(if( typedExpressionIR_cond ) statementIR_then)` be conditionalStatementIR
(Stmt_inst: p' IC' STO' |- statementIR_then ==> IC_1 STO_1 statementIR_then_inst)
     ...... Result in IC_1, STO_1, `(if( typedExpressionIR_cond ) statementIR_then_inst)` as statementIR

    ..... Case `conditionalStatementIR` matches pattern `IF(%)%ELSE%`
     ...... Let `(if( typedExpressionIR_cond ) statementIR_then else statementIR_else)` be conditionalStatementIR

   .... Let `_annotationList*` be the list of `_annotationList` and `_constantInitializerOptIR*` be the list of `_constantInitializerOptIR` and `_direction*` be the list of `_direction` and `_nameIR*` be the list of `_nameIR` and `typeIR*` be the list of `typeIR`, obtained by repeating:
    ..... Let `(_annotationList _direction typeIR _nameIR _constantInitializerOptIR)` be parameterIR
   .... for each `parameterIR` in `parameterIR*`
   .... Let `typeIR_package` be `(package< typeIR* >)` as typeIR
   .... Let `typeDefIR_package` be `(typeIR_package < typeParameterListIR , typeParameterListIR_inferred >)` as typeDefIR
   .... Let `IC_1` be `$add_typedef_inst((global), IC_0, nameIR, typeDefIR_package)`
   .... Let `cid` be `$cid_IR(nameIR, parameterIR*)`
   .... Let `constructor_package` be (package< typeParameterListIR ++ typeParameterListIR_inferred >( parameterIR* ))
   .... Let `IC_2` be `$add_constructor_inst((global), IC_1, cid, constructor_package)`
   .... Result in IC_2, STO', ?()

relation Decls_inst: p, IC', STO', declarationIR*

. Case analysis on declarationIR*
 .. Case `declarationIR*` matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p, IC, STO, []

   .... Result in IC', STO', []

 .. Case `declarationIR*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `declarationIR_h :: declarationIR_t*` be declarationIR*
  ... Group cons-none: p, IC_0, STO_0, declarationIR_h :: declarationIR_t*

(Decl_inst: p IC' STO' |- declarationIR_h ==> IC_1 STO_1 declarationIR'?)
   .... Assert that declarationIR'? matches pattern ()
(Decls_inst: p IC_1 STO_1 |- declarationIR_t* ==> IC_2 STO_2 declarationIR_t_inst*)
   .... Result in IC_2, STO_2, declarationIR_t_inst*
  ... Group cons-some: p, IC_0, STO_0, declarationIR_h :: declarationIR_t*

(Decl_inst: p IC' STO' |- declarationIR_h ==> IC_1 STO_1 declarationIR'?)
   .... Assert that declarationIR'? matches pattern (_)
   .... Let `?(declarationIR_h_inst)` be declarationIR'?
(Decls_inst: p IC_1 STO_1 |- declarationIR_t* ==> IC_2 STO_2 declarationIR_t_inst*)
   .... Result in IC_2, STO_2, declarationIR_h_inst :: declarationIR_t_inst*

relation Program_inst: p4program

. Group : p4program

(Program_ok: |- p4program : _typingContext (declarationIR* ;))
 .. Let `IC_0` be `$empty_instantiationContext`
 .. Let `STO_0` be `$empty_store`
(Decls_inst: (global) IC_0 STO_0 |- declarationIR* ==> IC_1 STO_1 declarationIR'*)
 .. Assert that declarationIR'* matches pattern []
 .. Result in IC_1.global.cenv, IC_1.global.tdenv, IC_1.global.renv, IC_1.global.venv, STO_1

relation Bind_arg: p_caller, IC_caller, p_callee, IC_callee_0, STO_0, id, argumentIR

. Group : p_caller, IC_caller, p_callee, IC_callee_0, STO_0, id, argumentIR

 .. Let `IC_caller_inner` be `$enter_path(IC_caller, id)`
(Argument_inst: p_caller IC_caller_inner STO_0 |- argumentIR ==> STO_1 value)
 .. Let `IC_callee_1` be `$add_value_inst(p_callee, IC_callee_0, id, value)`
 .. Result in IC_callee_1, STO_1

relation Bind_args: p_caller, IC_caller, p_callee, IC_callee', STO', id*, argumentIR*

. Case analysis on id*
 .. Case `id*` matches pattern []
  ... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern []
  ... Group nil: p_caller, IC_caller, p_callee, IC_callee, STO, [], []

   .... Result in IC_callee', STO'

 .. Case `id*` matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `id_h :: id_t*` be id*
  ... Assert that argumentIR* matches pattern _ :: _
  ... Let `argumentIR_h :: argumentIR_t*` be argumentIR*
  ... Group cons: p_caller, IC_caller, p_callee, IC_callee_0, STO_0, id_h :: id_t*, argumentIR_h :: argumentIR_t*

(Bind_arg: p_caller IC_caller | p_callee IC_callee' STO' |- id_h argumentIR_h ==> IC_callee_1 STO_1)
(Bind_args: p_caller IC_caller | p_callee IC_callee_1 STO_1 |- id_t* argumentIR_t* ==> IC_callee_2 STO_2)
   .... Result in IC_callee_2, STO_2

Let `_annotationList'*` be the list of `_annotationList'` and `_constantInitializerOptIR'*` be the list of `_constantInitializerOptIR'` and `_direction'*` be the list of `_direction'` and `_typeIR'*` be the list of `_typeIR'` and `id_cparam_default*` be the list of `id_cparam_default`, obtained by repeating:
